- **Edge cases:** Tiny months (4 rows) vs tall (6 rows) change the perimeter — compute from actual extents; atoms must not overlap day blocks or labels; determinism must survive week-start changes (seed on monthIndex, not on layout); with 5.2.8's targeted re-render, atoms shouldn't be rebuilt at all on event edits.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Atoms are "ambient life" (§5.5 line 562) — perimeter placement frames the month without obscuring data; reshuffling on every edit (current bug) is visually distracting.
- **Data flow notes:** Decorative — no event data; count/placement from `monthIndex` + grid extents only.
- **Testing notes:** Each cluster has 2–4 atoms at grid edges/corners; rebuilding produces identical atom positions (deterministic); atoms never appear in the 5.6 pick results.
- **Performance notes:** 24–48 atoms/year — must join the shared-geometry/instanced path (5.4) so they're cheap; deterministic placement also lets 5.2.8 *skip* rebuilding them on event edits.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Optionally fewer atoms / disabled on low-perf (`perfFlags.js`), same placement logic.
- **Integration points:** §5.3 line 512, §5.5 lines 561–563, 5.2.3, 5.2.8, 5.4, 5.6.
- **Status:** **Partial** (count 2–4 ✓; **placement random & center/behind**, **non-deterministic** across rebuilds). **Next:** seed placement by `monthIndex`, position on the grid perimeter, keep non-interactive, defer shared-geometry to 5.4.

## 5.2.7 — `InstancedMesh` optimization for day blocks (+ shared atom geometry)

- **Purpose:** Implement the §5.3 line 514-515 / §16.5 line 1479 instance optimization — merge same-material `DayBlock3D`s into `InstancedMesh` groups — which is the milestone's headline performance gap (~365 individual day blocks today).
- **Dependencies:** 5.3 (DayBlock3D geometry/material — what gets instanced), 5.2.4 (dimmed placeholders join a bucket), 5.2.6 (atoms share geometry); §5.3 lines 514–516, §16.5 lines 1479–1480.
- **Acceptance criteria:** `DayBlock3D` objects sharing the same material configuration (same category color + glass props) are rendered via **`InstancedMesh`** (or a documented equivalent batch) rather than one mesh each; the per-day **canvas-texture content** (§5.4) is still per-instance (instancing the panel geometry doesn't preclude per-instance content — resolved with 5.3); `AtomGlyph3D` shares one `SphereGeometry` + per-color material (§5.5 line 563); draw calls for a full year drop from hundreds to a small bounded number; frustum culling still removes off-screen clusters (§16.5 line 1480).
- **Implementation notes:** Today each day is `new DayBlock3D({...})` added to the cluster (WordWeaverScene.js:924–936); `useSharedMaterials: true` (930) already shares **materials** (half of §16.5's "pre-created and shared") but not geometry/draws. The hard part is §5.4's **per-day `CanvasTexture` content** (day number, summary, event rows) — `InstancedMesh` shares one material/texture, which fights per-instance text. Resolution options (decide with 5.3): (a) **two-layer** — instance the glass *panel* geometry (uniform, big win) and overlay per-day content as a separate lightweight textured quad / texture-atlas page indexed per instance; (b) **texture atlas** — one big texture with per-instance UV offset (instanced) holding each day's rendered canvas; (c) bucket by category color and accept per-bucket `InstancedMesh` for panels + individual content quads. Recommend (a)/(b): instance the panels (most of the geometry cost), keep content as an atlas or per-instance quad. Group by `{categoryColor, glassConfig, dimmed?}` so placeholders (5.2.4) and same-category days share buckets. Atoms: route through 5.4's shared `SphereGeometry`/`TorusGeometry` + per-color material. Measure with `renderer.info.render.calls` before/after.
- **Edge cases:** Per-instance emissive intensity for hover/focus (§5.4 line 532) under `InstancedMesh` needs per-instance attributes or a small overlay mesh for the active block; raycasting an `InstancedMesh` returns an `instanceId` (5.6 must map it back to a date); content updates (5.2.8) must update only the affected atlas page/quad, not the whole instanced buffer; dimmed placeholders in their own bucket; today-highlight as a per-instance attribute.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Invisible when right (same visuals, far fewer draws); the risk is per-instance hover/focus emphasis regressing — keep that crisp (overlay mesh for the focused block is a clean escape hatch).
- **Data flow notes:** Instancing is a render optimization over the same model reads; content still flows from `timelineModel` per day (5.2.8).
- **Testing notes:** `renderer.info.render.calls` for a full year is bounded (e.g. a handful of instanced buckets + labels + atoms, not ~400); hover/focus emphasis still works per day; raycast resolves `instanceId` → correct date; off-screen clusters are culled (draw count drops as the camera turns).
- **Performance notes:** **The milestone's primary perf win** — collapses ~365 day-block draws (+ atoms) into a few instanced draws; pairs with 5.2.8 (don't rebuild) and §16.2 LOD (5.2.9).
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Biggest payoff on mobile GPUs; gate any extra effects via `perfFlags.js`.
- **Integration points:** §5.3 lines 514–516, §16.5 lines 1479–1480, 5.3 (per-instance content), 5.2.4, 5.2.6, 5.2.8, 5.6 (instanceId picking).
- **Status:** **Missing the instancing half** (materials shared via `useSharedMaterials` ✓; geometry/draws **not** instanced — ~365 individual blocks). **Next:** instance the glass panels by material bucket, keep per-day content via atlas/overlay (decide with 5.3), share atom geometry (5.4), measure draw calls.

## 5.2.8 — Build/rebuild data flow & targeted canvas re-render (not full rebuild)

- **Purpose:** Reconcile the year layout's update path to §5.4 line 552 / §2.3 line 157 — on an event mutation, identify the **affected** `DayBlock3D`(s) and re-render **only their** canvas content (batched to next frame) — instead of today's full teardown+rebuild of all 12 clusters on a duplicate-bus signal.
- **Dependencies:** 5.1.9 (bus convergence to §13.2 + Phase-2 bus), 5.2.7 (instanced content update granularity), `timelineModel.js`; §5.4 line 552, §2.3 line 157, §13.2 lines 1160–1169.
- **Acceptance criteria:** On `eventCreated`/`eventUpdated`/`eventDeleted` (§13.2, Phase-2 bus), the layout maps the event date → the specific `DayBlock3D`(s) and **queues just their** content re-render, **batched to the next animation frame** — no full `build()`; `starterDataCleared`/`initialized` may still do a fuller rebuild; initial events and the today-injection of `getInitialNotes()` still populate correctly; all reads remain via `timelineModel.js` (§2.3 ✓).
- **Implementation notes:** Today `_onTimelineUpdated` → `_rebuildYearLayout()` → `_yearLayout.build(getInitialNotes())` (WordWeaverScene.js:156, 199) and `build()` runs `_clear()` + reconstruct-all (969–1000) — a single edit rebuilds 365 blocks **and reshuffles random atoms** (5.2.6). Reframe: (1) subscribe to the §13.2 catalog on the Phase-2 bus (5.1.9) instead of the lone `timelineUpdated`; (2) add a date→`DayBlock3D` index (extend `findDayBlock`, WordWeaverScene.js:1007, into a `Map<isoDate, block>`); (3) on a mutation, look up the affected day(s), mark them dirty, and in `update()`/next-frame flush re-render **only** those canvases (§5.4 line 552 batching) — reusing `enrichDayEvents(getEventsForDay(...))` (904) for just that day; (4) keep full `build()` for `initialized`/`starterDataCleared`/year change (5.2.10) only. The today-injection branch (`iso === today && !dayEvents.length`, 906–915) stays but applies per-day on re-render. This is the year-layout face of the recurring Phase-4/5 bus gap.
- **Edge cases:** An event whose date **moves** (update changes the day) dirties **two** days (old + new); deleting the last event of a day reverts it to empty/summary state; multiple mutations in one frame coalesce to one flush; events outside the displayed year (5.2.10) are ignored; `initialized` arriving before subscribe (5.1.9 boot-order note) → replay/rebuild once.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Targeted re-render makes edits feel instant and **stops the atom reshuffle/flash** a full rebuild causes; the 3D scene stays live with the 2D views (§2.3 line 157 "re-render visible portions").
- **Data flow notes:** `timelineModel` mutation → §13.2 event (Phase-2 bus) → date→block lookup → dirty set → next-frame canvas re-render of affected days → optional `*Focused` emit (5.2.9). Reads model-only.
- **Testing notes:** Editing one event re-renders exactly one (or two, on date-move) day canvas and triggers **no** full `build()` (spy on `build`); atom positions are unchanged after an edit (with 5.2.6 determinism); `renderer.info` shows no mass geometry churn per edit; `starterDataCleared` still rebuilds.
- **Performance notes:** Turns an O(365) rebuild-per-edit into O(1–2) canvas updates batched per frame — pairs with 5.2.7 (instanced) so even content updates touch one atlas page.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** The full-rebuild cost is most painful on mobile — this is a major mobile responsiveness fix.
- **Integration points:** §5.4 line 552, §2.3 line 157, §13.2 lines 1160–1169, 5.1.9, 5.2.7, 5.2.6, 5.2.10.
- **Status:** **Divergent** (full `build()` rebuild on the duplicate-bus `timelineUpdated`; no targeted/batched re-render; reads model-only ✓). **Next:** subscribe to §13.2 on the Phase-2 bus, index date→block, re-render only affected days batched to next frame, reserve `build()` for init/clear/year-change.

## 5.2.9 — LOD, frustum culling & month-focus support (year-overview perf + focus hooks)

- **Purpose:** Wire the year layout into §16's performance model (low-res `DayBlock3D` textures at year-overview distance, automatic frustum culling) and expose the month-focus hooks §13.2/§5.6 need (`monthClusters` API + `monthFocused {year, month}` emit).
- **Dependencies:** 5.2.7 (instancing/textures), 5.1.9 (bus for `monthFocused`/`navigateTo`), 5.5 (camera focus animation), 5.3 (DayBlock3D texture resolution tiers); §16.2 line 1317, §16.5 line 1480, §13.2 lines 1169/1171, §5.6 lines 587/599.
- **Acceptance criteria:** At year-overview camera distance, `DayBlock3D` canvas textures use the **low-res 128×160** tier (§16.2 line 1317), promoting to full res on focus; off-screen month clusters are **frustum-culled** (§16.5 line 1480 — verify Three.js auto-culling actually applies, i.e. correct bounding volumes); the layout exposes the handles for camera focus — `monthClusters[monthIndex]` (already present, WordWeaverScene.js:998/960) and `findDayBlock` (1007) — and, on a month/day focus action, the scene emits `monthFocused {year, month}` / `dayFocused {date}` (§13.2) on the Phase-2 bus and responds to `navigateTo {level}` (line 1171).
- **Implementation notes:** **LOD:** §16.2 line 1317 wants 128×160 textures at the year view; today day blocks build at one resolution (5.3 owns the canvas) — add a distance/zoom-driven tier so the overview is cheap and focus is crisp; the existing `update()` already computes per-block camera distance (`camPos.distanceTo(world)`, WordWeaverScene.js:1030) and toggles `setProximity` — reuse that signal to pick the texture tier. **Culling:** ensure each cluster/instanced mesh has a correct bounding sphere so Three's automatic frustum culling (§16.5 line 1480) drops the back/side clusters — test by watching `render.calls` as the camera turns. **Focus hooks:** the layout already exposes `monthClusters` + `findDayBlock` + `focusedDay`; the missing piece is **emitting** `monthFocused`/`dayFocused` (none emitted today — the 5.1.9 gap) and consuming `navigateTo` to set `focusedDay`/target a cluster. The actual camera *animation* is 5.5 (`focusOnMonth ~20 units out`, §5.6 line 599 / `focusOnDay`, line 598) — here just expose the targets and emit/consume the events.
- **Edge cases:** Texture tier thrashing as distance hovers at the threshold (hysteresis); a focused day must force full-res even mid-overview; culling must not drop a cluster that's partially on-screen (correct bounds); `navigateTo` to a month in a different year (5.2.10) has no target; emitting `monthFocused` on programmatic vs user focus (avoid feedback loops with 2D, per §15/line 1417–1418).
- **UI/UX considerations:** LOD keeps the 12-cluster overview smooth (§16 perf budget) while focus stays sharp; correct `*Focused` emits are what drive the Inkling panel's contextual summary (§13.2 consumer = InklingPanel).
- **Data flow notes:** Distance (camera) → texture tier; camera frustum → culling; user/`navigateTo` focus → `focusedDay`/cluster target → emit `monthFocused`/`dayFocused` → InklingPanel reacts (§13.2).
- **Testing notes:** At overview distance day textures are 128×160 and `render.calls` is low; turning the camera reduces draws (back clusters culled); focusing a day promotes its texture to full res; focusing a month emits `monthFocused {year, month}`; `navigateTo {level:"month"}` sets the right cluster target.
- **Performance notes:** LOD + culling + 5.2.7 instancing are the three legs of the §16 year-view budget; the proximity distance is already computed — reuse it (no extra cost).
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Lower texture tiers / fewer atoms on mobile (`perfFlags.js`); same focus hooks.
- **Integration points:** §16.2 line 1317, §16.5 line 1480, §13.2 lines 1169/1171, §5.6 lines 598–599, 5.1.9, 5.2.7, 5.5, 5.3.
- **Status:** **Missing/partial** (no texture LOD tier; culling unverified; focus *handles* exist but **no `*Focused` emit / `navigateTo` consume** — the 5.1.9 gap). **Next:** add a distance-driven texture tier (reuse the proximity distance), verify cluster bounds for culling, emit `monthFocused`/`dayFocused` and consume `navigateTo`.

## 5.2.10 — Year scope, non-spec extras, determinism & dispose completeness

- **Purpose:** Close the remaining correctness/cleanliness items so the year layout has a clean inverse and well-understood scope — single-year hardcoding, the non-spec week halos, layout determinism, and disposal completeness — and record the Phase-5 carry-forwards.
- **Dependencies:** 5.2.5 (label disposal), 5.2.6 (determinism), 5.2.8 (rebuild cadence), 5.1.9 (bus); §5.3, §16.5.
- **Acceptance criteria:** The hardcoded current-year (`new Date().getFullYear()`, WordWeaverScene.js:971) is documented as the current scope with a noted path for future year navigation (no multi-year nav exists — `navigateTo` across a year boundary is a known gap); the **week halos** (`WEEK_HALO_HEX`, non-spec) are explicitly kept-as-enhancement or trimmed, and counted in the instance/draw budget (5.2.7); the layout is **deterministic** across rebuilds (no `Math.random` — 5.2.6); `_clear()`/`dispose()` release **all** GPU resources created here (blocks, atoms, halos **and** the month-label textures/materials 5.2.5 flagged), verified leak-free across rebuilds; Phase-5 carry-forwards are recorded.
- **Implementation notes:** **Year scope:** `build()` always uses the current year (971) — fine for §5.3 ("the entire year") but note there's no year-change path; a future `navigateTo` to another year would need `build(year)` parameterization and the 5.2.8 full-rebuild path (one of the few legitimate full rebuilds). **Halos:** `WEEK_HALO_HEX` per-week planes (782, 862–878) aren't in §5.3 — decide keep (nice depth cue, already disposed in `_clear()` 1049–1053) vs trim; if kept, ensure they don't fight instancing (5.2.7) or LOD. **Determinism:** remove `Math.random()` from atom count/placement (5.2.6) so rebuilds and 5.2.8 targeted updates are stable. **Disposal:** `_clear()` (1037–1059) disposes blocks/atoms/halos and empties `root`, but the **month-label texture/material is leaked** (5.2.5) — add it; confirm `dispose()` (1061) → `_clear()` → `scene.remove(root)` is the documented inverse of `createYearLayout`. **Carry-forwards:** the duplicate `wordweaver/EventBus.js` + `preferenceChanged`/`modeChanged` catalog gaps (Phase-3/5.1.9), the week-start preference seam (5.2.3), and the spec's "4 or 5 rows" → "4–6" correction (5.2.3).
- **Edge cases:** Rebuild storms (5.2.8 should make these rare) must not leak label textures; trimming halos must not break the `_clear()` halo loop; a future year change mid-session must fully rebuild (atoms reseed deterministically per monthIndex, not per year — decide whether year should factor into the seed).
- **UI/UX considerations:** Determinism removes the distracting atom reshuffle; halos are a taste call (keep for depth, trim for minimalism per §5.3's clean intent).
- **Data flow notes:** None beyond the model-only reads already established.
- **Testing notes:** Rebuilding N times leaves `renderer.info.memory.textures`/`geometries` flat (no label/halo/atom leak); two builds with identical data produce identical positions (deterministic); `dispose()` removes `root` from the scene and zeroes GPU resources; year is the current calendar year.
- **Performance notes:** Disposal completeness prevents a slow texture leak under the (current) frequent-rebuild path; determinism enables 5.2.8 to skip rebuilding decorations.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Halos/atoms are first candidates to trim on low-perf (`perfFlags.js`).
- **Integration points:** §5.3, §16.5, 5.2.5, 5.2.6, 5.2.7, 5.2.8, 5.1.9, Phase-3 carry-forwards.
- **Status:** **Mixed** (single-year is in-scope but undocumented; **label texture leak** in `_clear()`; **non-deterministic** atoms; week halos are an undocumented non-spec extra — but blocks/atoms/halos *are* disposed). **Next:** document year scope, add label disposal, remove `Math.random`, decide halos, record carry-forwards.

<!-- source: phase-05-milestone-5.3-dayblock3d.md -->

# Phase 5 — Milestone 5.3: DayBlock3D (Expanded)

**Goal:** Specify the per-day glass tile (`DayBlock3D` — §5.4): a thin rounded glass panel (`RoundedBoxGeometry 2×2.5×0.15`, `MeshPhysicalMaterial` `transmission 0.7`/`roughness 0.1`/`metalness 0`/`thickness 0.5`) carrying a **single `CanvasTexture` map** of the day's content (day number top-left, AI daily summary ≤2 lines, **up to 3** event rows of icon+time+title, "…+N more", a ⏰ alert badge top-right), a three-state emissive machine (rest 0.25 / hover 0.6 / focus 0.9) with a +0.1 Y hover translation, a **queued, batched** canvas re-render (≤3 textures/frame), LOD texture tiers (128×160 overview / 256×320 month / 512×640 focus), and `InstancedMesh` panel support. This milestone is grounded against the **already-existing** [`DayBlock3D.js`](../../src/wordweaver/DayBlock3D.js) — so every task reconciles built / gap / next-step, not greenfield. It owns the per-day tile internals that 5.2 *positioned*; it does **not** cover ring assembly (5.2), atom internals (§5.5 → 5.4), camera focus animation (§5.6 → 5.5), or raycast/hover wiring (§5.7 → 5.6) — it exposes the hooks those consume.

**Spec alignment:** §5.4 (geometry `BoxGeometry(2,2.5,0.15)` + `RoundedBoxGeometry` corners, line 523-525; `MeshPhysicalMaterial` `transmission 0.7`/`roughness 0.1`/`metalness 0`/`thickness 0.5`, **no crack/fracture shaders**, line 528-530; `emissive` = dominant category color, `emissiveIntensity` 0.25/0.6/0.9 rest/hover/focus, line 531-532; **single `CanvasTexture` map overlay** updated on data change, line 534-535; canvas content = day number top-left grey, AI summary ≤2 lines white, **≤3** event rows icon+time+title, "…+N more", ⏰ top-right if any alert, line 537-542; resolution **256×320 (2× retina 512×640)**, line 544; state transitions incl. **+0.1 Y** on hover @150ms and focus camera-pan + day-detail panel, line 546-549; **content update** on `eventCreated/Updated/Deleted` → identify affected blocks, **queue canvas re-render batched to next frame**, line 551-552), §4.x line 427 (`generateDailySummary(date)` → 2–3 sentence NL summary used in DayBlock3D content), §16.1 line 1306-1307 (frame loop **processes the re-render queue, max 3 textures/frame**), §16.2 line 1311-1313 (the queue exists precisely to avoid bulk-update frame drops), §16.3 line 1317-1319 (LOD: 128×160 overview / 256×320 month / 512×640 focus, by camera distance), §16.5 line 1479 (same-dimension `DayBlock3D` panels share one `InstancedMesh`, category materials pre-created/shared), §22 line 1912 ("today" = a visually distinct `DayBlock3D` variant / glowing anchor), §20.x line 1632 (icons rasterized from the same SVG source at 16×16 into the canvas), §23 line 1653-1654 (hover 150ms ease-out / unhover 200ms ease-in), §5.7 line 610 (hover tooltip = day number + event count + dominant category).

**Sequencing:** Third milestone of Phase 5. It depends on 5.1's canonical scene/material conventions and 5.2's layout (5.2 places the blocks this milestone builds; 5.2.7's *scene-level* instancing is realized here via this milestone's *block-level* `InstancedMesh` panel support, resolving the per-day-content-vs-instancing tension 5.2.7 flagged as "decide with 5.3"; 5.2.8's *targeted re-render* feeds this milestone's *per-block* queued canvas update; 5.2.4's dimmed placeholders consume the empty/dimmed block variant defined here; 5.2.9's LOD tiers consume the texture-resolution tiers defined here). It feeds 5.4 (atom internals share the same shared-resource discipline), 5.5 (camera `focusOnDay` frames the block + reads its world position/today-anchor), and 5.6 (raycaster tests these panels, drives the hover/focus emissive states and the tooltip data this milestone exposes). It carries Phase 5's recurring findings: the **duplicate `wordweaver/EventBus.js`** drives an immediate full-label rebuild rather than the §13.2-bus queued re-render (5.1.9 / 5.2.8), and the per-day content path is the central efficiency reconciliation.

---

## Current implementation status (reconciliation)

`DayBlock3D` **already exists and is substantially built** ([`DayBlock3D.js`](../../src/wordweaver/DayBlock3D.js):196–430) — this is an upgrade/reconcile milestone. But it took a **fundamentally different rendering approach** than §5.4 in two big ways: it is a **flat plane**, not a thin glass box, and it renders content as **per-line billboarded `THREE.Sprite`s** (each with its own small `CanvasTexture`), not a **single `CanvasTexture` map** on the panel. The state model is also a **2-state camera-proximity** scheme, not the §5.4 3-state rest/hover/focus machine. The shared-resource scaffolding and an (unused) `InstancedMesh` panel class are present. Headline deltas vs §5.4/§16:

- **Geometry: flat `PlaneGeometry(1.2, 1.6)`, spec wants `RoundedBoxGeometry(2, 2.5, 0.15)`.** The panel is `new THREE.PlaneGeometry(PLANE_W=1.2, PLANE_H=1.6)` (DayBlock3D.js:5-6, 28, 242) — a **flat, zero-thickness** quad at the **wrong dimensions** (spec is 2×2.5, ~1.25× larger and a different aspect). There is **no thickness** (§5.4 wants 0.15 depth) and **no rounded corners** (`RoundedBoxGeometry` from `three/examples/jsm/geometries/`). The "glass box" is faked with three stacked planes: the panel, a `noteSurface` quad (`1.1×1.4`, z+0.006), and a backside `eventGlow` edge quad (`1.03×`, z−0.006). 5.3.1 reconciles to the rounded box. (Note: dimensions ripple into 5.2's `COL_SPACING`/`ROW_SPACING` footprint — coordinate with 5.2.1.)
- **Material: clearcoat fake-glass, no `transmission`/`thickness`, emissive lives on a *separate* halo mesh.** The panel `MeshPhysicalMaterial` (DayBlock3D.js:31-40) is `color #0a0a0a`, `opacity 0.92`, `roughness 0.15`, `metalness 0.05`, `clearcoat 1.0`, `clearcoatRoughness 0.1` — a dark glossy panel, **not** the spec's transmissive glass (`transmission 0.7`, `roughness 0.1`, `metalness 0`, `thickness 0.5`, line 528). The panel itself is **not emissive**; instead a backside `eventGlow` `MeshStandardMaterial` (264-272, `emissive` = category color, `emissiveIntensity 0.25`, `BackSide`, `opacity 0.35`) supplies the colored halo. §5.4 line 531 wants the **panel's** `emissive` set to the category color. The "no cracked/fracture shaders" rule (line 530) is satisfied (a `updateSharedCrackTime` no-op confirms crack shaders were removed, DayBlock3D.js:51-52 — keep). 5.3.2 reconciles material; 5.3.5 reconciles emissive states. Decide whether to keep the separate halo as an *enhancement* or move emissive onto the panel per spec.
- **Content: per-line `Sprite`s with individual `CanvasTexture`s, not one panel `CanvasTexture` map — the headline divergence.** §5.4 line 534-535 is explicit: a **single** `CanvasTexture` used as the panel's `map`, redrawn on data change. The code instead builds a **billboarded `THREE.Sprite` per line** — `createEventSprite` (DayBlock3D.js:131-146) and `createSummarySprite` (152-165) each call `createLabelTexture` (105-123) to make their **own** small canvas (256×64 / 280×56 / 300×48) → `CanvasTexture` → `SpriteMaterial` → `Sprite`. So a populated day is ~6–7 sprites, each a separate texture + draw, all camera-facing (quaternion-copied in `update`, 402-407). This **multiplies texture count** (~6/day × 365 ≈ thousands of small textures) and **defeats instancing** (5.3.7). 5.3.3 reconciles to the single-canvas model.
- **Summary is a count-string, not the AI daily summary.** `buildDaySummary` (DayBlock3D.js:170-178) returns `"{count} · {DominantCategoryLabel}"` (e.g. "3 · Work") — **not** the §5.4 line 539 / §4 line 427 "AI daily summary sentence (2–3 sentences, NL)" from `generateDailySummary(date)`. 5.3.3 wires the real summary (truncated to 2 lines).
- **Event-row count is 5, spec says 3; no "+N more"; alert is inline not a corner badge.** `MAX_SURFACE_LINES = 5` (DayBlock3D.js:10, sliced at 347) — spec line 540-541 caps visible rows at **3** with a "…+N more" indicator for the remainder (**absent** here). The ⏰ alert is applied **inline per event** (`_normalizeEvents`, 307-308: alarm/reminder/`alertId` → icon becomes ⏰), but §5.4 line 542 wants a **single ⏰ badge in the top-right corner** if *any* alert exists that day. Day number is a 📅 sprite (282-287), not the spec's "top-left small grey **number**". 5.3.4 reconciles rows + "+N more" + corner alert + day-number placement.
- **State model: 2-state camera-proximity, not 3-state rest/hover/focus.** `setProximity(near)` (DayBlock3D.js:378-385) is a **binary** driven by *camera distance* in the scene (`dist < 4.2 || isFocused`, WordWeaverScene.js:1032) — it sets target scale 1.08, glow 0.42, sprite opacity. §5.4 line 546-549 wants **three discrete** emissive levels (rest 0.25 / **hover** 0.6 / **focus** 0.9), a **+0.1 Y translation on hover**, 150ms-ease-out hover / 200ms-ease-in unhover (§23 line 1653-1654), with **hover driven by the raycaster** (5.6), not proximity. The emissive also currently animates on `eventGlow`, not the panel (5.3.2). 5.3.5 introduces the real machine (proximity can remain as an LOD/ambient cue, but hover/focus are raycast-driven).
- **`setEvents()` re-renders immediately; no queue, no batch, no ≤3/frame cap.** `setEvents` (DayBlock3D.js:366-373) calls `_rebuildSurfaceLabels()` **synchronously** — disposing and recreating all sprite textures inline. §5.4 line 552 + §16.1 line 1306-1307 + §16.2 line 1311-1313 want the scene to **queue** affected blocks and the **frame loop** to flush **≤3 canvas re-renders/frame**. No such queue exists (and the scene does a full rebuild anyway — 5.2.8). 5.3.6 introduces the per-block dirty/queue API consumed by the frame loop.
- **`InstancedMesh` panel class exists but is DEAD.** `DayBlockInstancedPanels` (DayBlock3D.js:57-97) is a complete instanced-panel helper (shared `panelGeo`/`panelMat`, `instanceColor`, `DynamicDrawUsage`, `finalize()`) — but **nothing constructs it**; the scene builds **individual** `new DayBlock3D` per day (WordWeaverScene.js:924). So §16.5 line 1479 instancing is *scaffolded but unused*, and the per-instance **content** problem (one InstancedMesh = one shared map, but each day needs its own canvas) is unsolved. This is the 5.2.7 "decide with 5.3" tension. 5.3.7 resolves it (instance the glass panel; carry per-day content via atlas/overlay).
- **Resolution is fixed; no retina 2×, no LOD tiers.** `createLabelTexture` uses fixed canvas sizes regardless of `devicePixelRatio` (§5.4 line 544 wants 256×320 base / **512×640 retina**), and there is **no LOD tier** (§16.3: 128×160 overview / 256×320 month / 512×640 focus). The scene computes per-block camera distance already (WordWeaverScene.js:1030) — the signal exists; the tiers don't. 5.3.8.
- **Today marker is faint; pick/tooltip data is thin.** `isToday` only bumps `eventGlow.emissiveIntensity` to 0.38 (DayBlock3D.js:291-293) — §22 line 1912 wants a **visually distinct today variant / anchor**. For picking, the group carries `userData.dayBlock = this` (224) and `name = day-block-${dateIso}` (223-224) — a good handle for 5.6 — but no exposed `{ dayNumber, eventCount, dominantCategory }` for the §5.7 line 610 hover tooltip. 5.3.9.
- **What's already right (keep):** `MeshPhysicalMaterial` chosen for the panel ✓ (just needs the glass params), **no crack/fracture shaders** ✓ (line 530, `updateSharedCrackTime` no-op), shared-resource path (`getSharedDayBlockResources`, `useSharedMaterials`) ✓, `classifyEvent`-derived icon/color/category/priority normalization ✓, model-only inputs (events passed in, no second source) ✓, billboarding math (`quaternion.copy(camera.quaternion)`) ✓ for whatever stays sprite-based, smooth `lerp` toward target scale/glow ✓, sprite/edge/label **disposal** present ✓ (411-429), `userData.dayBlock` + stable `name` pick handle ✓, `DayBlockInstancedPanels` scaffold ✓ (just wire it).

---

## 5.3.1 — Geometry: rounded glass box (`RoundedBoxGeometry 2×2.5×0.15`)

- **Purpose:** Reconcile the tile geometry to §5.4 line 523-525 — a **thin rounded box** `2 × 2.5 × 0.15` with `RoundedBoxGeometry` corners — replacing the current flat `PlaneGeometry(1.2, 1.6)`, so the day reads as a physical glass panel with depth, not a paper-thin quad.
- **Dependencies:** 5.1 (Three.js loads via import map → confirm `three/examples/jsm/geometries/RoundedBoxGeometry.js` resolves on the unpkg `three@0.164.0` build, the 5.1.10 vendoring concern), 5.2.1 (cluster footprint — the 2×2.5 size changes `COL_SPACING`/`ROW_SPACING` math); §5.4 line 523-525.
- **Acceptance criteria:** The panel is a `RoundedBoxGeometry(2, 2.5, 0.15, segments, radius)` (small corner radius, e.g. 0.08, low segment count for the year-scale budget); the front face hosts the content `CanvasTexture` (5.3.3); the geometry is created **once** and shared via `getSharedDayBlockResources()` for non-focused blocks; dimensions are named constants; the new footprint is fed back to 5.2.1's spacing so clusters still don't collide.
- **Implementation notes:** Replace `PLANE_W=1.2`/`PLANE_H=1.6` and `new THREE.PlaneGeometry(...)` (DayBlock3D.js:5-6, 28, 242) with `RoundedBoxGeometry(2, 2.5, 0.15, 2, 0.08)` imported from `three/examples/jsm/geometries/RoundedBoxGeometry.js`. Collapse the three-plane stack (panel + `noteSurface` + backside `eventGlow`) decision: with a real box + transmissive material (5.3.2) and a panel-`map` (5.3.3), the separate `noteSurface` quad becomes redundant (content goes on the box front face) — retire it; the `eventGlow` halo is a keep-or-trim call shared with 5.3.2/5.3.5. Map the content canvas onto the box's front face (UVs of `RoundedBoxGeometry` cover all faces — either accept the map on all faces or use a thin separate front-face quad for the canvas; document the choice). Update `COL_SPACING`/`ROW_SPACING` in WordWeaverScene.js (5.2.1) for the 2×2.5 footprint.
- **Edge cases:** `RoundedBoxGeometry` UVs differ from a plane — verify the content map lands upright on the front face (may need a dedicated front quad); high segment/radius counts blow the year budget (~365 boxes) — keep segments minimal; the 1.25× size bump must not reintroduce the 5.2.1 cluster-overlap problem at radius 80; if the import fails offline (5.1.10), fall back to a chamfered box or document the vendoring need.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Depth + rounded corners are what sell "glass tile" vs "flat sticker"; the front face must remain flat enough to read the canvas content head-on.
- **Data flow notes:** Pure geometry; content/material are 5.3.2/5.3.3.
- **Testing notes:** Panel bounding box is ~2×2.5×0.15; corners are rounded (not square); one shared geometry instance is reused across non-focused blocks (`renderer.info.memory.geometries` doesn't scale with day count for the shared path); clusters at radius 80 don't overlap with the new footprint.
- **Performance notes:** A low-segment rounded box is marginally heavier than a plane per-vertex but shared geometry keeps it to one buffer; the real budget lever is instancing (5.3.7), not this.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Same geometry; consider lower corner segments on low-perf (`perfFlags.js`).
- **Integration points:** §5.4 line 523-525, 5.1.10 (jsm vendoring), 5.2.1 (footprint/spacing), 5.3.2 (material on this geometry), 5.3.3 (content map face), 5.3.7 (instanced shared geo).
- **Status:** **Divergent** (flat `PlaneGeometry(1.2,1.6)`, no thickness, no rounded corners, wrong dimensions; faked with a 3-plane stack). **Next:** swap to shared `RoundedBoxGeometry(2,2.5,0.15)`, retire the redundant `noteSurface` quad, feed the new footprint to 5.2.1.

## 5.3.2 — Glass material (`MeshPhysicalMaterial` transmission/roughness/metalness/thickness)

- **Purpose:** Reconcile the panel material to §5.4 line 527-530 — true transmissive glass (`transmission 0.7`, `roughness 0.1`, `metalness 0.0`, `thickness 0.5`) with the **panel's own `emissive`** set to the dominant category color — replacing the current dark clearcoat fake-glass + separate emissive halo.
- **Dependencies:** 5.3.1 (the geometry it clads), 5.3.5 (emissive intensity is animated there), 5.1 (renderer must enable what `transmission` needs — `MeshPhysicalMaterial` transmission requires the renderer's transmission render target; verify on the r164 build); §5.4 line 527-532, §19 line 1552 (`--3d-glass-transmission` token 0.0–1.0).
- **Acceptance criteria:** Non-focused/shared blocks use a `MeshPhysicalMaterial` with `transmission: 0.7`, `roughness: 0.1`, `metalness: 0.0`, `thickness: 0.5` (a clean glass look); the material's **`emissive`** is the day's dominant category color with `emissiveIntensity` per the 5.3.5 state (0.25 rest); **no** crack/fracture/distortion shaders (line 530 ✓ — keep); the glass transmission value is sourced from / consistent with the `--3d-glass-transmission` CSS token (§19 line 1552) where feasible; the shared material is reused for like-colored blocks (instancing bucket, 5.3.7).
- **Implementation notes:** Rewrite the shared `panelMat` (DayBlock3D.js:31-40) and the per-block fallback (230-241): set `transmission: 0.7`, `roughness: 0.1`, `metalness: 0.0`, `thickness: 0.5`, drop the opaque `color #0a0a0a`/`opacity 0.92`/`clearcoat` fake-glass (a faint tint + `ior ~1.5` is fine). Move `emissive` onto **this** material (currently on the backside `eventGlow`, 264-272) — set `emissive` to the category color, `emissiveIntensity` driven by 5.3.5. Decide the `eventGlow` halo's fate: with panel emissive, it's redundant — **retire** it, or keep as a deliberate extra (then document and budget it). **Transmission caveat:** `MeshPhysicalMaterial.transmission > 0` requires a transmission render pass (extra cost) and an environment/background to refract — on ~365 blocks this is **expensive**; recommend transmission only for the **focused/near** block (5.3.8 LOD) and a cheaper opaque-emissive approximation for far/instanced blocks (the InstancedMesh material in 5.3.7 typically can't do per-instance transmission anyway). Document this perf-driven split explicitly.
- **Edge cases:** Transmission with `InstancedMesh` (5.3.7) is effectively unsupported per-instance — far blocks must use a non-transmissive shared material (the split above); transmission needs something behind the glass to refract (the year ring / environment) — verify it isn't just black; `thickness 0.5` vs the 0.15 box depth is a *material* thickness param (refraction depth), independent of geometry depth — keep as spec; emissive color must update on `setEvents` (5.3.3) when the dominant category changes.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Glass transmission is the signature look (§5.1) but is the single biggest GPU risk at year scale — the focused-only transmission split preserves the look where it's seen up close while keeping the overview cheap.
- **Data flow notes:** `emissive` color derives from the day's dominant category (from normalized events); intensity from the 5.3.5 state; transmission/roughness/metalness/thickness are constant (token-driven).
- **Testing notes:** Focused block shows transmissive glass (refraction visible); far blocks use the cheap emissive material; `emissive` equals the dominant category color and updates when events change; no crack/fracture shader code paths remain; `renderer.info.render.calls`/frame time with transmission limited to the focused block stays within the §16.4 budget.
- **Performance notes:** Transmission is the costliest material feature here — gate it to the focused/near block; share opaque-emissive materials per category for the rest (ties to 5.3.7/5.3.8).
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Disable transmission entirely on low-perf/mobile (`perfFlags.js`) — fall back to the opaque-emissive glass approximation everywhere; desktop gets focused-block transmission.
- **Integration points:** §5.4 line 527-532, §19 line 1552, 5.3.1, 5.3.5 (emissive intensity), 5.3.7 (instanced material can't transmit), 5.3.8 (transmission only when focused), `perfFlags.js`.
- **Status:** **Divergent** (clearcoat fake-glass, no `transmission`/`thickness`; emissive on a separate backside halo, not the panel; no-crack rule ✓). **Next:** adopt transmissive glass params, move `emissive` onto the panel, split transmission to focused-only for perf, decide the halo's fate.

## 5.3.3 — Single `CanvasTexture` content map (day number + AI summary)

- **Purpose:** Reconcile content rendering to §5.4 line 534-539 — **one** `CanvasTexture` used as the panel `map`, drawn as a 2D canvas (day number top-left grey, **AI daily summary** ≤2 lines white) — replacing the current per-line `Sprite` + count-string-summary approach.
- **Dependencies:** 5.3.1 (the face the map clads), 5.3.4 (event rows draw into the same canvas), 5.3.8 (the canvas size = LOD tier), §4 line 427 (`generateDailySummary`); §5.4 line 534-539.
- **Acceptance criteria:** Each populated day renders its content into a **single** `<canvas>` → `CanvasTexture` → assigned as the panel material's `map` (one texture/draw per block, not ~6 sprites); the canvas draws the **day number** top-left (small, light grey), then the **AI daily summary** (`generateDailySummary(date)`, §4 line 427) wrapped/truncated to **2 lines** (white, small); the texture is redrawn (not recreated) on data change where possible (`tex.needsUpdate = true`) and disposed on block dispose; empty days render a minimal canvas (just the day number) and dimmed days (5.2.4) a near-blank one.
- **Implementation notes:** Replace the sprite pipeline (`createEventSprite`/`createSummarySprite`/`createLabelTexture` + the `surfaceLabels`/`_sprites` group, DayBlock3D.js:131-165, 278-289, 328-360) with a **single canvas draw** function `drawDayCanvas(ctx, { day, summary, events, hasAlert, lodTier })`. Allocate one `<canvas>` at the LOD-tier size (5.3.8), draw: clear → day number top-left (`fillStyle` grey, small bold) → AI summary white wrapped to 2 lines (measure + truncate with "…") → event rows (5.3.4) → "+N more" (5.3.4) → ⏰ corner (5.3.4). Build the `CanvasTexture` once, set as `panelMat.map` (or a dedicated front-face quad's map if 5.3.1 uses one); on update, re-run the draw into the **same** canvas and set `texture.needsUpdate = true` (avoid new GPUTexture allocs). **Summary source:** replace `buildDaySummary` (count-string, 170-178) with `generateDailySummary(date)` (§4 line 427) — if that AI call is async/expensive, draw the cheap count-string immediately and **upgrade** the canvas when the real summary resolves (and only for near/focused tiers per 5.3.8). Keep `_normalizeEvents` (300-320) as the icon/color/category/priority source.
- **Edge cases:** Long summaries — measure and truncate to exactly 2 lines with an ellipsis; non-Latin/emoji metrics in `measureText`; async summary arriving after the block disposed (guard); empty day = day-number-only canvas; the day-number must not collide with the ⏰ corner badge (5.3.4) or the summary; retina scaling — draw at the LOD pixel size and set `texture` filters (`LinearFilter`) as today (105-122).
- **UI/UX considerations:** A single legible card per day (number → summary → events) reads far better than stacked billboard sprites, and stays readable at month-focus distance; the AI summary is the "what happened this day" glance value (§5.1).
- **Data flow notes:** `day`/`dateIso` (static) + `generateDailySummary(date)` (AI, §4) + normalized events (5.3.4) → one canvas → one `CanvasTexture` map. Reads events from the array passed by the scene (model-only upstream, §2.3).
- **Testing notes:** A populated day has **one** content texture (not ~6); the canvas shows day number + a 2-line summary + rows; updating events redraws the **same** canvas (`needsUpdate`) without allocating a new texture (`renderer.info.memory.textures` flat across edits); the summary text comes from `generateDailySummary`, not a count-string.
- **Performance notes:** Collapsing ~6 sprite textures/day into 1 canvas/day is a **major** texture-count and draw-call reduction (~thousands → ~hundreds) and is the precondition for instancing (5.3.7) and the ≤3/frame queue (5.3.6).
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Smaller canvas tier on mobile; the AI summary may be skipped or shortened on low-perf.
- **Integration points:** §5.4 line 534-539, §4 line 427, 5.3.1, 5.3.4, 5.3.6 (redraw-in-place), 5.3.7 (single map enables atlas), 5.3.8 (canvas size).
- **Status:** **Divergent** (per-line `Sprite`s with individual `CanvasTexture`s; summary is a count-string not the AI summary). **Next:** render one canvas → one panel `map`, draw day number + 2-line `generateDailySummary`, redraw in place on update.

## 5.3.4 — Event rows, "…+N more", and the ⏰ alert badge

- **Purpose:** Reconcile the event listing to §5.4 line 540-542 — **up to 3** event rows (category icon + time + truncated title), a "…+N more" indicator beyond 3, and a **single ⏰ badge in the top-right corner** when *any* alert exists for the day — drawn into the single canvas (5.3.3).
- **Dependencies:** 5.3.3 (the canvas they draw into), `classifyEvent`/`getCategoryColor` (already wired), §20 line 1632 (16×16 SVG-sourced icons); §5.4 line 540-542.
- **Acceptance criteria:** The canvas shows at most **3** event rows (today's `MAX_SURFACE_LINES = 5` corrected to **3**), each as `icon + time + title` with the title truncated to fit; if `events.length > 3`, a "**…+N more**" line is drawn (N = `length − 3`); a **⏰** badge appears in the **top-right corner** iff any event that day has an alert (`alertId`/`kind alarm|reminder`) — *not* per-row; icons are rasterized from the shared 16×16 SVG source (§20 line 1632) where available (emoji fallback acceptable, documented); rows use the per-category color.
- **Implementation notes:** In `drawDayCanvas` (5.3.3): take `events.slice(0, 3)`; for each draw `icon` (16×16), `time` (if present), then `title` truncated via `measureText` + "…"; if `events.length > 3` draw "…+{events.length−3} more". Compute `hasAlert = events.some(e => e.alertId || e.kind === "alarm" || e.kind === "reminder")` and, if true, draw a ⏰ glyph in the **top-right** corner (distinct from the inline behavior in `_normalizeEvents` 307-308, which made the row icon ⏰ — keep alert-row classification for color/category, but the *corner badge* is the day-level indicator). Change `MAX_SURFACE_LINES = 5` → a `MAX_VISIBLE_ROWS = 3` constant. For icons: §20 line 1632 wants the **same SVG source** rasterized at 16×16 into the canvas — load/rasterize the category SVGs once into an offscreen atlas and `drawImage` per row (cache); emoji glyphs (current 📅/⏰/•) are an acceptable documented fallback if the SVG atlas isn't ready.
- **Edge cases:** Exactly 3 events = no "+N more"; 4 events = "+1 more"; very long titles must truncate without overflowing the canvas width; time-less events (all-day) omit the time segment; the corner ⏰ must not overlap the top-left day number or the summary; multiple alerts = still one badge; category color contrast on the glass background (ensure legibility).
- **UI/UX considerations:** 3 rows + "+N more" keeps the tile uncluttered and matches the 2D month-cell rule (§24 line 1378 / Phase-4.2's "+N more" popover) — consistency across 2D/3D; the corner ⏰ is an at-a-glance "this day has a reminder" cue.
- **Data flow notes:** Normalized events (icon/time/title/category/color/alert) → rows + N-more + corner badge, all into the 5.3.3 canvas. Tapping "+N more"/the block routes to day detail (5.6 / §13 line 684).
- **Testing notes:** A day with 5 events shows 3 rows + "…+2 more"; a day with an alarm shows the corner ⏰ exactly once; titles truncate with "…"; icons render at 16×16; row colors match category.
- **Performance notes:** All within the single 5.3.3 canvas draw — no extra textures/draws; the SVG atlas is rasterized once and cached.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Fewer rows or smaller text on the smallest LOD tier (5.3.8); same logic.
- **Integration points:** §5.4 line 540-542, §20 line 1632, §24 line 1378 (2D "+N more" parity), 5.3.3, 5.3.8, 5.6 (tap → day detail).
- **Status:** **Partial/divergent** (rows exist but capped at **5** not 3; **no "+N more"**; alert is **inline per-row**, not a corner badge; day number is a 📅 sprite; icons are emoji not SVG-rasterized). **Next:** cap at 3 rows, add "+N more", draw a single top-right ⏰ badge, rasterize 16×16 SVG icons (emoji fallback).

## 5.3.5 — Emissive state machine (rest / hover / focus) + hover Y-translation

- **Purpose:** Reconcile the visual-state model to §5.4 line 546-549 + §23 line 1653-1654 — three discrete emissive levels (**rest 0.25 / hover 0.6 / focus 0.9**) on the **panel**, a **+0.1 Y** translation on hover, and 150ms-ease-out hover / 200ms-ease-in unhover transitions — replacing the current binary camera-proximity scheme.
- **Dependencies:** 5.3.2 (emissive lives on the panel now), 5.6 (raycaster sets hover/focus), 5.5 (focus triggers camera pan + day-detail panel); §5.4 line 546-549, §23 line 1653-1654, §5.7 line 609-613.
- **Acceptance criteria:** The block exposes a state API — `setHovered(bool)` and `setFocused(bool)` (or a `setState('rest'|'hover'|'focus')`) — that drives the **panel** `emissiveIntensity` to **0.25 / 0.6 / 0.9** respectively; entering hover also translates the group **+0.1 in Y** and animates over **150ms ease-out**; leaving hover animates back over **200ms ease-in**; focus is sticky until cleared (and triggers the 5.5 camera pan + day-detail open, owned there); the existing camera-distance `setProximity` is **demoted** to an ambient/LOD cue (or removed) so it no longer masquerades as hover.
- **Implementation notes:** Today `setProximity(near)` (DayBlock3D.js:378-385) is the only state hook and it's distance-driven from the scene (WordWeaverScene.js:1032). Introduce explicit `setHovered`/`setFocused` setting `_targetEmissive` to 0.25/0.6/0.9 and `_targetY` to base/base+0.1; in `update()` (392-409) lerp `emissiveIntensity` and `position.y` toward targets with **time-constants matching 150ms (hover) / 200ms (unhover)** rather than the current generic `Math.min(1, delta*8)`. Move the emissive target onto the **panel** material (5.3.2), not `eventGlow`. **Hover must be raycast-driven (5.6), not proximity** — the §5.7 model intersects the panel each frame; keep `setProximity`/distance only for LOD (5.3.8) or a subtle far/near scale, clearly separated from hover. Focus state persists and coordinates with 5.5 (`focusOnDay`) — the block just reflects `focused`; the camera/panel are the scene's job. Preserve the smooth-lerp pattern already present.
- **Edge cases:** Hover→focus→unhover ordering (focus should outrank hover for emissive); rapid hover flicker at panel edges (debounce/hysteresis in 5.6); the +0.1 Y must compose with 5.2's grid position and any bob, not fight it; instanced blocks (5.3.7) need per-instance emissive — hover/focus may use an **overlay mesh** for the active block (the clean escape hatch noted in 5.2.7) since `InstancedMesh` can't cheaply do per-instance emissive intensity; today-variant (5.3.9) baseline emissive must not be overwritten by "rest 0.25".
- **UI/UX considerations:** The three-tier emissive + lift is the core tactile feedback of the 3D view (§5.7); 150/200ms asymmetric easing (fast in, slower out) is the spec's polish — match it.
- **Data flow notes:** Raycaster (5.6) → `setHovered`/`setFocused` → target emissive/Y → `update()` lerp; focus also → 5.5 camera/panel. No model data involved.
- **Testing notes:** Hover sets panel `emissiveIntensity → 0.6` and `position.y += 0.1` over ~150ms; unhover returns over ~200ms; focus sets `0.9` and is sticky; rest is `0.25`; proximity no longer changes emissive on its own.
- **Performance notes:** Per-frame lerp on the hovered/focused block(s) only is negligible; the instanced-overlay approach avoids per-instance attribute churn.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** No hover on touch — mobile goes rest→focus on tap (the §5.7 line 613 "tap focuses; tap again opens detail"); the +0.1 Y/hover tier is desktop-pointer only.
- **Integration points:** §5.4 line 546-549, §23 line 1653-1654, §5.7 line 609-613, 5.3.2, 5.3.7 (per-instance emissive/overlay), 5.5 (focus→camera/panel), 5.6 (raycast→hover/focus), 5.3.9 (today baseline).
- **Status:** **Divergent** (binary camera-**proximity** scale/glow, not 3-state rest/hover/focus; emissive on the halo not the panel; no +0.1 Y; generic ease, not 150/200ms; hover not raycast-driven). **Next:** add `setHovered`/`setFocused` → panel emissive 0.25/0.6/0.9 + +0.1 Y, match 150/200ms easing, demote proximity to LOD.

## 5.3.6 — Content update: queued, batched canvas re-render (≤3/frame)

- **Purpose:** Reconcile the update path to §5.4 line 551-552 + §16.1 line 1306-1307 + §16.2 line 1311-1313 — affected blocks are **queued** and the **frame loop** flushes their canvas re-render **batched, max 3 textures/frame** — replacing today's synchronous full-sprite-rebuild in `setEvents`.
- **Dependencies:** 5.3.3 (redraw-in-place canvas), 5.2.8 (scene maps event→affected block(s) and marks dirty), 5.1.9 (subscribe to §13.2 `eventCreated/Updated/Deleted` on the Phase-2 bus); §5.4 line 552, §16.1 line 1306-1307, §16.2 line 1311-1313.
- **Acceptance criteria:** A block exposes `markDirty()` (enqueue) instead of re-rendering inline; the scene maintains a **re-render queue**; the frame loop (§16.1 step 6) processes **at most 3** dirty blocks/frame, redrawing each one's canvas (5.3.3, in place via `needsUpdate`) and clearing its dirty flag; a bulk change (e.g. `starterDataCleared`, many events) drains over several frames **without a frame drop** (§16.2); `setEvents` updates the block's data + emissive color but **defers the draw** to the queue.
- **Implementation notes:** Split today's `setEvents` (DayBlock3D.js:366-373): keep the data update (`_normalizeEvents`, dominant color → panel emissive 5.3.2) but replace the immediate `_rebuildSurfaceLabels()` with `this._dirty = true` (or push to a scene queue). Add a scene-level `_renderQueue` (Set of dirty blocks); in the frame loop (WordWeaverScene `_tick`), after updates, `for up to 3 dirty blocks: block.redrawCanvas(); dequeue`. `redrawCanvas` re-runs `drawDayCanvas` (5.3.3) into the existing canvas + `texture.needsUpdate = true`. Wire enqueue to 5.2.8's date→block map on §13.2 events (5.1.9) — on `eventUpdated` whose date moved, mark **both** old+new days dirty. The §16.1 frame-loop ordering (step 6 = process queue, before render) should be honored. This is the per-block half of 5.2.8's targeted-re-render reconciliation.
- **Edge cases:** More than 3 days change in one frame → remainder flush next frame(s) (cap holds); a block disposed while dirty → remove from queue; the same block marked dirty twice in a frame coalesces (Set semantics); focused block should arguably **jump the queue** (redraw immediately for responsiveness) — decide and document; LOD tier (5.3.8) at redraw time picks the right canvas size.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Batching keeps edits smooth even under bulk import/clear (§16.2's whole point); a single edit still appears within a frame or two (imperceptible).
- **Data flow notes:** §13.2 `eventCreated/Updated/Deleted` (Phase-2 bus, 5.1.9) → 5.2.8 date→block(s) → `markDirty` → frame-loop flush ≤3 → `redrawCanvas` + `needsUpdate`. Reads model-only.
- **Testing notes:** Editing one event marks exactly one (or two, on date-move) block dirty and redraws it within ≤2 frames; bulk-clearing N>3 days drains at ≤3/frame with no frame exceeding the §16.4 budget; `setEvents` no longer redraws synchronously (spy: no canvas draw outside the flush); texture is reused (`needsUpdate`), not reallocated.
- **Performance notes:** The ≤3/frame cap (§16.2) is the explicit anti-frame-drop guard; pairs with 5.3.3 (cheap in-place redraw) and 5.2.8 (no full rebuild).
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Lower the per-frame cap (e.g. 2) on low-perf to protect mobile frame time (`perfFlags.js`).
- **Integration points:** §5.4 line 552, §16.1 line 1306-1307, §16.2 line 1311-1313, 5.3.3, 5.2.8, 5.1.9, §16.4 budget.
- **Status:** **Missing** (`setEvents` rebuilds sprite textures **synchronously**; no queue, no ≤3/frame batch; scene does a full rebuild anyway — 5.2.8). **Next:** add `markDirty`/`redrawCanvas`, a scene re-render queue flushing ≤3/frame, wire to §13.2 via 5.2.8.

## 5.3.7 — `InstancedMesh` panels + per-instance content (wire the dead scaffold)

- **Purpose:** Realize §5.4 line 515 / §16.5 line 1479 — same-dimension `DayBlock3D` panels share one `InstancedMesh`, category materials pre-created/shared — by **wiring the existing-but-unused `DayBlockInstancedPanels`** and resolving the per-instance **content** problem (one InstancedMesh = one shared map) that 5.2.7 deferred to here.
- **Dependencies:** 5.3.1 (shared box geometry), 5.3.2 (shared materials, transmission caveat), 5.3.3 (single canvas → atlas-able), 5.2.7 (scene-level instancing decision), 5.6 (raycast → `instanceId` → date); §5.4 line 514-516, §16.5 line 1479-1480.
- **Acceptance criteria:** Day **panels** of the same dimensions/material bucket render via `DayBlockInstancedPanels` (`InstancedMesh`, already coded at DayBlock3D.js:57-97) instead of one `Mesh` each; per-day **content** is carried via a **texture atlas** (one big texture, per-instance UV offset) **or** a lightweight per-day overlay quad — documented choice — so instancing doesn't force one shared canvas; category color is applied per-instance (`instanceColor`, already supported, 67-78); a full year drops to a small bounded draw-call count; raycasting returns an `instanceId` mapped back to a date (5.6); off-screen clusters frustum-cull (§16.5 line 1480 — but note `frustumCulled = false` is currently set at line 64, which **defeats** culling — reconcile).
- **Implementation notes:** The scaffold exists: `DayBlockInstancedPanels` (capacity 400, shared `panelGeo`/`panelMat`, `instanceColor`, `setMatrixAt`/`setColorAt`, `finalize`). Wire the scene (5.2.7) to build **one** instanced-panels object per material bucket and `add(matrix, categoryHex)` per day instead of `new DayBlock3D` per day (WordWeaverScene.js:924). **Content tension (the 5.2.7 hand-off):** an `InstancedMesh` shares **one** material/map, but each day needs its own 5.3.3 canvas — resolve via (a) **atlas**: render each day's canvas into a tile of one large atlas texture and give each instance a per-instance UV-offset attribute (instanced custom attribute + a tiny shader tweak), or (b) **overlay quads**: instance the glass panels (the bulk geometry/lighting cost) and place a cheap per-day textured quad in front for content (more draws than atlas, simpler). Recommend **atlas** for the year overview (cheapest) with a **full `DayBlock3D` upgrade for the focused/near block** (5.3.8) so focus gets transmission (5.3.2) + crisp 512×640 (5.3.8) — i.e. instanced+atlas for the many, a real mesh for the one. **Fix `frustumCulled = false`** (line 64): with the whole year in one InstancedMesh, per-cluster culling is lost — either keep per-cluster InstancedMeshes (so each cluster culls) or compute a correct bounding volume; reconcile with §16.5 line 1480 and 5.2.9. Hover/focus emissive (5.3.5) on an instance → use the overlay/real-mesh for the active block. Measure `renderer.info.render.calls` before/after.
- **Edge cases:** Atlas capacity (≥365 + placeholders) vs max texture size — page the atlas if needed; per-instance UV-offset requires a custom shader/`onBeforeCompile` (document); `instanceId` → date map must survive re-render (5.3.6 updates an atlas tile, not the whole buffer); dimmed placeholders (5.2.4) = their own bucket/dim material; per-instance transmission is unsupported → far instances use opaque-emissive (5.3.2); capacity 400 must cover 6-row months + placeholders (could exceed 400/year across buckets — size per bucket).
- **UI/UX considerations:** Invisible when right (identical look, far fewer draws); the focused-block-as-real-mesh keeps the up-close glass/transmission quality the instanced path can't match.
- **Data flow notes:** Layout (5.2) computes per-day matrices + category hex → `DayBlockInstancedPanels.add`; content (5.3.3) → atlas tile per instance; raycast `instanceId` → date (5.6). Render optimization over the same model reads.
- **Testing notes:** Full-year `render.calls` is bounded (handful of buckets + atlas + labels/atoms, not ~400); each day shows its own content (atlas tile correct); per-instance category color correct; raycast returns the right date; turning the camera culls off-screen clusters (`render.calls` drops) — confirming the `frustumCulled` fix.
- **Performance notes:** **The headline year-scale perf win** alongside 5.3.3 — collapses ~365 panel draws into a few instanced draws; atlas keeps content to one texture; focused-block real-mesh is the one exception.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Biggest payoff on mobile GPUs; the atlas path especially. Gate transmission/overlay extras via `perfFlags.js`.
- **Integration points:** §5.4 line 514-516, §16.5 line 1479-1480, 5.2.7, 5.3.1, 5.3.2, 5.3.3, 5.3.8 (focused real-mesh), 5.6 (`instanceId`), 5.2.9 (culling).
- **Status:** **Scaffolded but dead** (`DayBlockInstancedPanels` fully coded, **nothing constructs it**; scene builds individual blocks; `frustumCulled=false` defeats culling; per-instance content unsolved). **Next:** wire the instanced panels per material bucket, carry content via atlas (focused block = real mesh), fix culling, map `instanceId`→date.

## 5.3.8 — Canvas resolution, retina, and LOD tiers (128×160 / 256×320 / 512×640)

- **Purpose:** Implement §5.4 line 544 + §16.3 line 1317-1319 — the content canvas resolution follows a **LOD tier** by camera distance (128×160 year-overview, 256×320 month-focus, **512×640** day-focus) with a **2× retina** base (512×640 when `devicePixelRatio > 1`) — none of which exists today (fixed-size sprite canvases).
- **Dependencies:** 5.3.3 (the canvas being sized), 5.2.9 (scene LOD/distance signal), 5.3.7 (atlas tile size = tier), 5.3.2 (focused tier enables transmission); §5.4 line 544, §16.3 line 1315-1321.
- **Acceptance criteria:** The content canvas (5.3.3) is allocated at the tier matching the block's camera distance — **128×160** at year-overview, **256×320** at month-focus, **512×640** at day-focus (and 512×640 as the retina base for the focused block); tier selection uses the per-block camera distance already computed in the scene (`update()`, WordWeaverScene.js:1030); crossing a tier boundary **redraws** the canvas at the new size (via the 5.3.6 queue) with **hysteresis** to avoid thrashing; the focused block is always full-res regardless of a momentary distance reading.
- **Implementation notes:** Today `createLabelTexture` uses fixed sizes (256×64 etc., DayBlock3D.js:105) with **no DPR scaling and no tiers**. Define tier constants `{ overview:[128,160], month:[256,320], focus:[512,640] }`; in the scene LOD pass (5.2.9, reuse the `camPos.distanceTo` at WordWeaverScene.js:1030), assign each block a tier and, on change, enqueue a redraw (5.3.6) at the new canvas size. For the **atlas** path (5.3.7), the overview tile size is 128×160 (keeps the atlas small); the focused real-mesh (5.3.7) uses 512×640. Retina: when `devicePixelRatio > 1`, the focus tier is already 512×640 (the spec's retina target) — apply DPR only up to the focus tier (don't 2× the overview, defeats the LOD saving). Add hysteresis (separate enter/exit distance thresholds) so a block hovering at a boundary doesn't redraw every frame.
- **Edge cases:** Tier thrash at boundaries (hysteresis required); a focused block momentarily far (mid-animation) must stay full-res (focus overrides distance); atlas tiles are fixed-size — promoting a single block to 512×640 means pulling it out of the atlas into the real-mesh path (5.3.7); memory: 512×640 × focused-only is fine, but 512×640 × 365 would blow VRAM — enforce focus-only for the top tier; DPR>2 capped at 2 (mirror 5.1's `min(dpr,2)`).
- **UI/UX considerations:** Overview stays crisp-enough at 128×160 (text is summary-level), focus is sharp at 512×640 — the LOD is invisible done right and is the §16 perf budget's third leg (with instancing 5.3.7 and the queue 5.3.6).
- **Data flow notes:** Per-block camera distance (scene) → tier → (on change) enqueue redraw at tier size (5.3.6) → canvas/atlas updated. No model data.
- **Testing notes:** At overview distance blocks use 128×160; focusing one promotes it to 512×640; tiers switch with hysteresis (no per-frame redraw at a boundary); `renderer.info.memory.textures` reflects mostly small tiles + one full-res focused texture, not 365 large ones.
- **Performance notes:** LOD is a large VRAM + fill-rate saver at year scale; reuses the existing distance computation (no new cost); the ≤3/frame queue (5.3.6) absorbs tier-change redraws.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Cap the top tier lower on mobile (e.g. 256×320) and skip retina doubling (`perfFlags.js`).
- **Integration points:** §5.4 line 544, §16.3 line 1315-1321, 5.3.3, 5.2.9, 5.3.6, 5.3.7, 5.1 (`min(dpr,2)`).
- **Status:** **Missing** (fixed sprite-canvas sizes; no DPR/retina handling; no LOD tiers). **Next:** add tier constants + distance-driven tier selection (reuse the scene distance), redraw on tier change via the queue with hysteresis, focus-only top tier.

## 5.3.9 — Today variant + pick/tooltip data exposure

- **Purpose:** Implement §22 line 1912 (a **visually distinct** today `DayBlock3D` variant / glowing anchor) and expose the per-block data the §5.7 line 610 hover **tooltip** and 5.6 picking need — beyond today's faint `isToday` emissive bump and bare `userData.dayBlock`.
- **Dependencies:** 5.3.5 (today baseline must compose with the emissive machine), 5.6 (raycast reads the exposed data), 5.5 (`focusOnToday` targets the today block); §22 line 1912, §5.7 line 610, §5.6 line 600 (`focusOnToday`).
- **Acceptance criteria:** Today's block is **visually distinct** (a stronger glow/accent ring/anchor per §22 line 1912 — not just `emissiveIntensity 0.38`), and its distinct treatment is the **baseline** the 5.3.5 rest/hover/focus deltas apply on top of (today rest > normal rest); the block exposes a stable pick handle (`userData.dayBlock` + `name = day-block-${dateIso}` — already present, keep) **and** a lightweight `getTooltipData()` → `{ dayNumber, eventCount, dominantCategory }` (§5.7 line 610); `findDayBlock`/the date→block map (5.2.8) can locate today for `focusOnToday` (5.5).
- **Implementation notes:** Today: `isToday` only sets `eventGlow.emissiveIntensity = 0.38` (DayBlock3D.js:291-293). Upgrade to a clearer anchor — e.g. a brighter rest emissive on the panel (5.3.2/5.3.5), an accent outline ring, or the §22 "glowing anchor" treatment; make today's **rest** emissive a distinct baseline so hover/focus still read as deltas above it. Expose `getTooltipData()` returning `{ dayNumber: this.day, eventCount: this.events.length, dominantCategory: <from buildDaySummary/normalized events> }` for the 5.6 tooltip (§5.7 line 610 wants day number + event count + dominant category, appearing after 500ms hover per §21 line 1775). Keep `userData.dayBlock = this` and the stable `name` (223-224) — they're the pick handles 5.6/5.2.8 rely on. For instancing (5.3.7), the today block is a natural candidate for the **real-mesh** exception (always distinct + likely focused).
- **Edge cases:** Today changes at midnight (a long-lived session) — the today flag should be refreshable (tie to 5.2.10 year/day scope); today + focused + hover state precedence (focus/hover deltas above the today baseline); today in a non-current year (none, single-year scope 5.2.10) — N/A today; tooltip data must reflect post-edit counts (recompute on `setEvents`/redraw 5.3.6).
- **UI/UX considerations:** "Today" is the primary spatial anchor in the year ring (§22) — it must be findable at a glance from overview distance; the tooltip is the quick hover read (§5.7/§21).
- **Data flow notes:** `isToday` (from scene, date compare) → distinct baseline; `getTooltipData()` derives from `day` + normalized events → 5.6 tooltip; pick handle (`userData`/`name`) → 5.6 raycast / 5.2.8 map / 5.5 `focusOnToday`.
- **Testing notes:** Today's block is visibly distinct at overview distance; hover/focus still increase emissive above the today baseline; `getTooltipData()` returns correct day number + live event count + dominant category; the today block is locatable via the date→block map for `focusOnToday`.
- **Performance notes:** Negligible (one distinct block); if it's the real-mesh exception (5.3.7) that's one extra draw.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Tooltip is pointer-hover (desktop); mobile shows the same data via tap/long-press context (§25 line 1736) — the exposed data serves both.
- **Integration points:** §22 line 1912, §5.7 line 610, §21 line 1775, §5.6 line 600, 5.3.5, 5.3.7, 5.2.8, 5.5, 5.2.10 (today refresh).
- **Status:** **Partial** (faint `isToday` glow bump; pick handle `userData.dayBlock`/`name` ✓; **no distinct today anchor**, **no tooltip data accessor**). **Next:** give today a distinct baseline treatment that composes with 5.3.5, add `getTooltipData()`, keep the pick handles.

## 5.3.10 — Disposal, shared-resource lifecycle, no-crack invariant & carry-forwards

- **Purpose:** Close the correctness/cleanliness items so the tile has a clean inverse and shared resources are managed once — disposal completeness across the new canvas/atlas/instanced paths, the shared-resource singleton lifecycle, the no-crack-shader invariant, and Phase-5 carry-forwards.
- **Dependencies:** 5.3.3 (canvas texture), 5.3.7 (atlas/instanced), 5.3.2 (shared materials), 5.2.10 (scene-level dispose/determinism); §5.4 line 530, §16.5.
- **Acceptance criteria:** `dispose()` releases **all** GPU resources a block owns — the content `CanvasTexture` (5.3.3) and any owned material (non-shared path) — without disposing **shared** resources (`getSharedDayBlockResources` singletons are disposed **once** at scene teardown, not per block); the atlas/instanced resources (5.3.7) are disposed by the scene, not individual virtual days; the **no cracked/fracture/distortion shader** invariant (§5.4 line 530) is preserved (the `updateSharedCrackTime` no-op stays as a tombstone); rebuilding the year leaves `renderer.info.memory.{textures,geometries}` **flat** (no per-day texture leak — the central win of moving off per-line sprites); Phase-5 carry-forwards are recorded.
- **Implementation notes:** Today `dispose()` (DayBlock3D.js:411-429) correctly guards shared vs owned (`_ownsPanelMat`/`_ownsNoteMat`/`_ownsEdgeGeo`, `_useShared`) and disposes sprite textures, edge mat, day label — **good discipline, keep the pattern**. As the rendering model changes: (1) dispose the **single content `CanvasTexture`** (5.3.3) here instead of N sprite textures; (2) the `noteSurface` (retired in 5.3.1) and `eventGlow` halo (retired/kept in 5.3.2) disposal lines must track those decisions; (3) **shared singletons** (`_sharedResources`, DayBlock3D.js:19-49) currently have **no disposer** — add a `disposeSharedDayBlockResources()` called once at scene teardown (5.2.10/5.1 dispose), and ensure individual blocks never dispose shared geo/mats (the `_useShared` guards already prevent this — verify after the material rewrite); (4) the atlas + `InstancedMesh` (5.3.7) are scene-owned — dispose in the scene's `_clear()`/`dispose()` (5.2.10). **No-crack invariant:** keep `updateSharedCrackTime` as a no-op tombstone (51-52) and add a test/lint note that no fracture/distortion shader is reintroduced (§5.4 line 530). **Carry-forwards:** the duplicate `wordweaver/EventBus.js` vs Phase-2 bus (5.1.9), the missing §13.2 `dayFocused` emit on focus (5.5/5.6), the `generateDailySummary` async-cost handling (5.3.3), and the §16.5 `frustumCulled=false` culling fix (5.3.7).
- **Edge cases:** Frequent rebuilds (until 5.2.8/5.3.6 land) must not leak the content texture — dispose on every teardown; shared-singleton disposal must happen **after** all blocks are gone (order); atlas pages (5.3.7) disposed even if partially filled; a block disposed mid-redraw (5.3.6 queue) must be removed from the queue and not redraw a freed canvas.
- **UI/UX considerations:** None directly — this is correctness; but the single-canvas move (5.3.3) is what stops the texture-count explosion that would otherwise degrade the whole scene over time.
- **Data flow notes:** None beyond resource lifecycle.
- **Testing notes:** Creating + disposing N blocks leaves `renderer.info.memory.textures`/`geometries` flat (no leak); shared resources persist across block disposal and are freed exactly once at scene teardown; no fracture/distortion shader exists; the content texture is disposed on block dispose.
- **Performance notes:** Disposal completeness prevents a slow leak under the (current) frequent-rebuild path; the single-canvas model caps texture count.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Leaks hurt mobile VRAM first — disposal completeness matters more there.
- **Integration points:** §5.4 line 530, §16.5, 5.3.2, 5.3.3, 5.3.7, 5.2.10, 5.1.9, carry-forwards.
- **Status:** **Partial** (per-block dispose discipline ✓ incl. shared/owned guards; **no shared-singleton disposer**; disposal must follow the single-canvas/atlas rewrite; no-crack invariant ✓). **Next:** dispose the single content texture, add `disposeSharedDayBlockResources()` at teardown, keep the no-crack tombstone, record carry-forwards.


---

<!-- source: phase-05-milestone-5.4-atomglyph3d.md -->

# Phase 5 — Milestone 5.4: AtomGlyph3D (Expanded)

**Goal:** Specify the decorative ambient atom (`AtomGlyph3D` — §5.5): a **slowly rotating, non-interactive** structure of a central `SphereGeometry(0.3, 16, 16)` (`MeshStandardMaterial`, slight emissive) plus **2–3 `TorusGeometry` rings** of varying radii on randomized axes, each ring rotating at a distinct slow speed (**0.002–0.008 rad/frame**), placed **2–4 per month cluster at the corners/edges of the day grid**, all instances sharing a **single `SphereGeometry` reference + two `TorusGeometry` references** with **materials shared per-color**. Purpose is visual depth / ambient life only — no interactive function. This milestone is grounded against the **already-existing** [`AtomGlyph3D.js`](../../src/wordweaver/timeline3d/AtomGlyph3D.js) (the live year-ring atom, via `createBackground`) and the two **parallel** atom implementations [`AtomGlyph.js`](../../src/wordweaver/AtomGlyph.js) and [`WordWeaverAtomOrbits.js`](../../src/wordweaver/WordWeaverAtomOrbits.js) — so every task reconciles built / gap / next-step, not greenfield. It owns the decorative-atom internals and their cluster placement; it does **not** cover the day tile (5.3), ring assembly / cluster layout (5.2 — which *positions* atoms via `populateMonthCluster` and explicitly **deferred shared-atom-geometry to here** in 5.2.6), camera (§5.6 → 5.5), or raycast/hover (§5.7 → 5.6) — it must simply stay *out* of the pick path (5.4.8).

**Spec alignment:** §5.5 line 554-563 (decorative atom = central sphere `SphereGeometry(0.3, 16, 16)` `MeshStandardMaterial` slight emissive, line 558; **2–3** `TorusGeometry` rings of varying radii at different axis orientations, line 559; each ring rotates at a **distinct slow speed 0.002–0.008 rad/frame** around a **randomized axis**, line 560; **2–4 per month cluster at the corners or edges** of the day grid, line 561; **no interactive function**, line 562; **all instances share a single `SphereGeometry` + two `TorusGeometry` refs via instancing, materials shared per-color**, line 563), §5.3 line 512 (2–4 `AtomGlyph3D` decorations floating around the **cluster perimeter**), §5.3 line 516 (`AtomGlyph3D` objects use a **single shared `SphereGeometry` and shared `MeshStandardMaterial`** — the instance-optimization restatement), §1.4 line 88 (aesthetic choices incl. atom glyphs must **never compromise readability or performance**), §11 line 146 + §11 line 1010 + §28 line 1963 (the canonical path is **`src/wordweaver/AtomGlyph3D.js`** and the tree shows `AtomGlyph3D × 2–4` under each `MonthCluster`), §5.8 line 631 (subtle floating `AtomGlyph3D` elements **outside** month clusters as ambient scene decoration), §16.1 line 1306 (frame-loop step 5 = "Animate `AtomGlyph3D` rotations"), §23.2 line 1656 ("AtomGlyph rotate | Continuous | ∞ | linear"), §23.3 line 1665 (under `prefers-reduced-motion`, **`AtomGlyph3D` rotation is paused**), §27.6 line 1821 (3D continuous motion incl. AtomGlyph rotations **fully paused** under reduced motion).

**Sequencing:** Fourth milestone of Phase 5. It depends on 5.1's canonical scene/material/dispose conventions and on 5.2's cluster layout (5.2's `populateMonthCluster` is where atoms are created and placed; **5.2.6 explicitly deferred shared-geometry + deterministic perimeter placement to this milestone**). It mirrors 5.3's shared-resource discipline (module-level shared geometry/material singletons + a guarded disposer that never frees shared refs — the 5.3.10 lesson applies directly). It feeds 5.5 (camera motion must not make atoms appear to track the user — they stay world-fixed in the cluster) and 5.6 (the raycaster must **exclude** atoms — 5.4.8 is the contract 5.6 consumes). It carries two Phase-5 recurring threads: the **duplicate-renderer / parallel-stack** pattern (here it's **three** atom classes — `timeline3d/AtomGlyph3D.js` live in the year ring, root `AtomGlyph.js`, and `WordWeaverAtomOrbits.js` in the satellite weave — echoing Phase 4's two-week-renderer and 5.1's two-scene findings), and **non-determinism / no-instancing** as the central performance reconciliation (the `Math.random`-driven, per-instance-allocated atoms are the §1.4/§16 risk this milestone closes).

---

## Current implementation status (reconciliation)

A decorative atom **already exists and is live in the year ring** — but it is a *much heavier* object than §5.5 describes, it is **allocated per-instance with no sharing/instancing**, it is **placed non-deterministically** with `Math.random`, and it **ignores `prefers-reduced-motion`**. There are also **three** parallel atom implementations, only one of which is wired into the Phase-5 year ring. This is an upgrade/reconcile + converge milestone, not greenfield.

- **Three parallel atom classes; the spec path doesn't match the live file.** §11 line 146/1963 and the §11 tree (line 1010) cite **`src/wordweaver/AtomGlyph3D.js`**, but the file that is actually **live in the year ring** is **`src/wordweaver/timeline3d/AtomGlyph3D.js`** ([AtomGlyph3D.js](../../src/wordweaver/timeline3d/AtomGlyph3D.js):6), constructed via `AtomGlyph3D.createBackground(...)` in `populateMonthCluster` ([WordWeaverScene.js](../../src/wordweaver/WordWeaverScene.js):884) and updated/disposed through `WordWeaverYearLayout3D.bgAtoms` (WordWeaverScene.js:957, 1019, 1044-1047). Two other atoms exist and are **not** in the year ring: root **[`AtomGlyph.js`](../../src/wordweaver/AtomGlyph.js)** (a gold rings+nucleus+orbiting-particles glyph, `class AtomGlyph`, no current importer in the year path), and **[`WordWeaverAtomOrbits.js`](../../src/wordweaver/WordWeaverAtomOrbits.js)** (the **satellite-weave** decoration — pink "feminine palette" per-note atoms, gated on `getActivePalette().atomOrbits`, mounted on `weaveGroup` at WordWeaverScene.js:428-429, a *different* subsystem from the §5.5 cluster atom). 5.4.1 picks the canonical class and reconciles the path; 5.4.10 dispositions the other two.
- **Geometry is far over-spec: a 64×64 glass shell + four high-seg wave rings + three electrons, not "sphere(0.3,16,16) + 2–3 tori".** §5.5 line 558-559 wants a simple central `SphereGeometry(0.3, 16, 16)` + **2–3** `TorusGeometry` rings. The live atom (AtomGlyph3D.js:25-103) is a **"premium aqua orb"**: a `SphereGeometry(0.225, **64, 64**)` shell on an expensive `MeshPhysicalMaterial` (`transmission: 1.0`, `thickness: 0.7`, `clearcoat: 1.0`, `ior: 1.45`), **four** `TorusGeometry(r, t, 48, 96)` "wave rings" (`ringSpecs`, 60-65), **plus three** orbiting electron spheres (`SphereGeometry(0.0225,16,16)`, 84-103, each with a **cloned** material). That is dramatically heavier than the spec's lightweight decoration (transmission alone forces a render-target pass; 64×64 + 4×(48×96) tori is a large vertex/triangle budget for an *ambient* element repeated 24–48× across the ring). 5.4.2 reconciles geometry/material toward the spec; 5.4.3 shares it.
- **No shared geometry / no instancing — the headline perf gap 5.2.6 deferred here.** §5.5 line 563 + §5.3 line 516 are explicit: **one shared `SphereGeometry` + two shared `TorusGeometry` refs**, materials shared per-color, via instancing. The code instead calls `new AtomGlyph3D({...})` per atom (WordWeaverScene.js:884), and each constructor **allocates fresh** `shellGeo`, four ring geometries, and an electron geometry, and **clones** an electron material per electron (AtomGlyph3D.js:96 `eMat.clone()`). With **2–4 atoms × 12 clusters = ~24–48 atoms**, that is dozens of distinct geometries + materials that should be a handful of shared refs. 5.4.3 introduces module-level shared singletons (mirroring 5.3's `getSharedDayBlockResources`), the direct realization of 5.2.6's deferral.
- **Placement is non-deterministic `Math.random`, not deterministic grid corners/edges.** §5.5 line 561 / §5.3 line 512 want **2–4 atoms at the corners or edges (perimeter)** of the day grid. `populateMonthCluster` (WordWeaverScene.js:880-895) sets `atomCount = 2 + Math.floor(Math.random()*3)` and randomizes **scale** (`2 + Math.random()*3`), **opacity** (`0.1 + Math.random()*0.1`), and **position** (`(Math.random()-0.5)*9`, `-Math.random()*weekCount*ROW_SPACING*0.4`, `-2.5 - Math.random()*2.5`) — so atoms float at **random interior/behind** positions (z pushed back 2.5–5 units, plus `createBackground` forces `z=-6` by default, AtomGlyph3D.js:122), **not** the cluster perimeter, and the layout **changes every rebuild** (non-reproducible, defeats any cache, and visually unstable across the full-rebuild path of 5.2.8). This is the same determinism+perimeter concern 5.2.6 raised and deferred. 5.4.4 makes placement deterministic and perimeter-anchored.
- **Rotation animation is delta-based "premium" motion, not the spec's per-frame slow-spin on randomized axes.** §5.5 line 560 wants each of the 2–3 rings to rotate at a **distinct slow speed (0.002–0.008 rad/frame)** around a **randomized axis**.
