- **Purpose:** Reconcile mobile controls to §5.6 line 577-583 — a **custom touch handler** (single-drag orbit, pinch zoom, two-finger pan, thumb-slide fly, fly/descend buttons) **not** `OrbitControls` — versus the live combination of `OrbitControls` touch **plus** the `WordWeaverMobileControls` overlay (forward pad, joystick, Fly/Descend buttons).
- **Dependencies:** 5.5.1 (single camera authority), 5.5.6 (mobile fly feeds the same single fly-input sink), 5.1 (scene/controls init); §5.6 line 577-583, §11 line 147, §27 (touch ergonomics).
- **Acceptance criteria:** The mobile interaction covers all spec gestures — single-finger drag orbit, two-finger pinch zoom, two-finger drag pan, thumb-slide fly-forward, hold-to-fly / hold-to-descend buttons; a decision is recorded between **(a)** formalizing the working `OrbitControls`-touch + `WordWeaverMobileControls`-overlay combo as "the mobile handler" (cheaper, already covers the gestures) and updating §5.6 line 577 to acknowledge OrbitControls handles orbit/pinch/pan on mobile, **or (b)** building the dedicated `TouchController.js` the spec names (and disabling OrbitControls touch); the mobile fly/joystick/altitude input routes into the **same** single fly-input sink as desktop (5.5.6), not a parallel one; the overlay shows only on touch/mobile (`shouldShowWordWeaverMobileControls`, WordWeaverMobileControls.js:8) ✓.
- **Implementation notes:** The overlay already exists and is wired (`WordWeaverChrome` constructs it, sums `getInput()` into `setFlightInput`, WordWeaverChrome.js:26-29/145-150) and provides forward pad + joystick + Fly/Descend (WordWeaverMobileControls.js:42-58) ✓. The **gap** vs §5.6 is only that orbit/pinch/pan come from `OrbitControls` (which the spec says mobile shouldn't use). Recommend **(a)**: keep OrbitControls for touch orbit/pinch/pan (it's robust and free) + the overlay for fly/altitude, and reconcile the spec wording — building a bespoke `TouchController` to re-implement orbit/pinch/pan is high-cost for parity with what OrbitControls already does. If **(b)** is required, gate OrbitControls `enableRotate/Zoom/Pan` off on mobile and implement the gestures in `TouchController`. Ensure the overlay's fly input and OrbitControls don't both consume the same touch (the overlay is a separate DOM layer — confirm pointer capture doesn't leak to the canvas).
- **Edge cases:** Two-finger pinch (OrbitControls zoom) vs the forward pad (fly) must not both fire from one gesture — the pad is a dedicated DOM element with its own pointer capture (WordWeaverMobileControls.js:79) ✓, but verify a pinch starting over the pad doesn't get swallowed; the native shell (`inkling-native-shell`, WordWeaverMobileControls.js:11) forces the overlay on — confirm that's intended for Tauri; reduced-motion (5.5.10) doesn't disable controls but does snap focus moves; the overlay must hide when leaving 3D mode (WordWeaverChrome.js:62 `setEnabled(false)`) ✓.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Large hold-targets for fly/descend + a slide pad are good touch ergonomics (§27); re-implementing orbit/pinch from scratch risks worse feel than OrbitControls — favor (a) unless the spec parity is mandatory.
- **Data flow notes:** Touch → OrbitControls (orbit/zoom/pan) + overlay `getInput()` → `setFlightInput` → single fly sink (5.5.6) → camera.
- **Testing notes:** On a touch device: single-drag orbits, pinch zooms, two-finger pans, slide-pad flies forward, Fly/Descend buttons change altitude; the overlay shows only on mobile/touch; fly input and OrbitControls don't fight; leaving 3D hides the overlay.
- **Performance notes:** Overlay is DOM (cheap); OrbitControls touch is the same cost as desktop. No extra RAF.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** This is the mobile path; desktop uses 5.5.5/5.5.6. Both feed the same fly sink (5.5.6).
- **Integration points:** §5.6 line 577-583, §11 line 147, §27, 5.5.1, 5.5.6 (shared fly sink), `WordWeaverMobileControls.js`, `WordWeaverChrome.js`.
- **Status:** **Partial / spec-deviation.** Gestures are covered, but via `OrbitControls` touch + a custom overlay, not the spec's standalone `TouchController`. **Next:** record decision (a) formalize the combo + reconcile §5.6 wording, or (b) build `TouchController` and disable OrbitControls touch; route mobile fly into the single sink.

## 5.5.8 — Complete the focus API (`focusOnToday`/`focusOnNextEvent`/`focusOnCategory`) + day-panel trigger

- **Purpose:** Reconcile the focus API to §5.6 line 597-602 — add the **three missing** methods (`focusOnToday`, `focusOnNextEvent`, `focusOnCategory`) and reconcile **`focusOnDay`'s day-detail-panel behavior** (spec line 598 says it opens the panel; §5.7 line 613 says a *second* click opens it — settle the rule with 5.6) — so the camera has the full documented navigation surface that the top bar, "today" button, and category nav can call.
- **Dependencies:** 5.5.1 (the methods live on the canonical camera/scene), 5.2 (`findDayBlock`/`monthClusters` resolve targets), 5.5.9 (these emit §13.2 events), 5.6 (click vs focus panel rule), Phase-1/timelineModel (next-event / category queries read the model); §5.6 line 597-602, §5.7 line 613.
- **Acceptance criteria:** `focusOnToday()` computes today's month/day and calls `focusOnDay` (handling "today not in the rendered year" gracefully); `focusOnNextEvent()` queries the model for the **next upcoming** event (≥ now) and `focusOnDay`s its date (no-op or gentle feedback if none); `focusOnCategory(category)` finds the **next day containing** an event of that category and focuses it (cycling on repeat calls — "next" each time); the **day-panel trigger is settled** — either `focusOnDay` opens the panel (spec line 598) **or** focus-then-second-click opens it (§5.7 line 613) — pick one, document, and make `focusOnDay` consistent with it (today it opens **nothing**, WordWeaverScene.js:226-234); all methods are no-throw on missing targets.
- **Implementation notes:** Add the three methods next to `focusOnDay`/`focusOnMonth` (WordWeaverScene.js:214-234) on the canonical owner (5.5.1); `focusOnToday` = `focusOnDay(todayMonth, todayDate)` guarded for the rendered year; `focusOnNextEvent` reads the model's event list (timelineModel — the Phase-1 store, **model-only** per the 5.1 read-only invariant) sorted by start, first ≥ now; `focusOnCategory` reuses the `classifyEvent`/category normalization (the 5.3.x classifier) to scan forward from the current focus for the next matching day, tracking a cursor so repeated calls advance. Wire the day-panel decision: if "focus opens panel," call the day-panel open after the move snaps (5.5.4); if "second click opens," leave `focusOnDay` panel-less and let 5.6's click logic open it on the already-focused block (§5.7 line 613).
- **Edge cases:** Today outside the rendered year (the ring shows one year) → `focusOnToday` should either reframe to the nearest in-range or no-op with feedback; no upcoming events / no events of a category → graceful no-op; `focusOnCategory` cursor must wrap and not infinite-loop on a single matching day; opening the panel mid-move vs after-snap affects perceived responsiveness (prefer after-snap, or open immediately and let the camera catch up — document); these methods are the API the **top bar / keyboard / "Today" button** call (§13.2) — keep signatures stable.
- **UI/UX considerations:** "Jump to today / next event / next deadline of category" are core navigation affordances; without them the 3D mode can only be browsed manually. The panel-trigger rule must be unambiguous so click and programmatic focus feel consistent.
- **Data flow notes:** `focusOnToday`/`NextEvent`/`Category` read the **model** (Phase-1) → resolve a date → `focusOnDay` → camera move + (per rule) day panel; emit §13.2 (5.5.9).
- **Testing notes:** Each method moves the camera to the correct target (today, next event's day, next category day); repeated `focusOnCategory` advances; missing-target cases no-op cleanly; the day panel opens per the settled rule (and only then); methods read model, never mutate it.
- **Performance notes:** Next-event/category scans are O(events) on the already-loaded model — cheap; cache the sorted event list if called frequently.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Identical API; invoked from platform-appropriate UI (top bar, buttons, keyboard).
- **Integration points:** §5.6 line 597-602, §5.7 line 613 (panel rule), 5.2 (target resolution), 5.5.9 (event emit), 5.6 (click panel logic), Phase-1 timelineModel (queries), 5.3 classifier (category).
- **Status:** **Incomplete.** Only `focusOnMonth`/`focusOnDay` exist; `focusOnToday`/`focusOnNextEvent`/`focusOnCategory` are **missing**; `focusOnDay` opens **no** panel (spec line 598 / §5.7 line 613 unreconciled). **Next:** add the three methods (model-only reads), settle and wire the day-panel trigger.

## 5.5.9 — §13.2 bus integration: emit `dayFocused`/`monthFocused`, consume `navigateTo`

- **Purpose:** Close the recurring Phase-4/5 bus gap for the camera: per §13.2 line 1418, a 3D navigation must update `focusedDate` and **emit `dayFocused`/`monthFocused`**; per §13.2 line 436, `WordWeaverScene` must **listen to `navigateTo`** so a 2D-side or top-bar navigation moves the 3D camera — neither happens today (focus is bus-silent and the scene listens only to the duplicate WordWeaver bus's `timelineUpdated`).
- **Dependencies:** 5.1.9 (bus convergence onto the Phase-2 `src/utils/EventBus.js`), 5.5.8 (the focus methods that emit), 5.6 (pick → focus → emit chain); §13.2 line 1418/436/1101-1102/1278, §13.2 catalog.
- **Acceptance criteria:** `focusOnDay` emits **`dayFocused { date }`** (and updates the canonical `focusedDate`) on the Phase-2 bus; `focusOnMonth` emits **`monthFocused { monthIndex }`**; the scene **subscribes to `navigateTo`** (§13.2 line 436) and routes `level:"day"` → `focusOnDay`, `level:"month"` → `focusOnMonth`, `level:"week"` → (defer/closest-day) — so top-bar / 2D navigation drives the 3D camera; emissions go on the **canonical** bus (5.1.9), not the duplicate `src/wordweaver/EventBus.js`; no event storm (emit once per settled navigation, not per lerp frame — emit on focus *start* or *snap*, document which).
- **Implementation notes:** In the canonical focus methods (5.5.8), after setting the target, `bus.emit("dayFocused", { date })` / `bus.emit("monthFocused", { monthIndex })`; subscribe the scene to `navigateTo` (the same listener seam 5.1.9 sets up) and map `level` → focus method (mirroring §13.2 line 1101-1102/1278 exemplars). Emit on navigation **commit** (focus call), not inside `_updateCameraFocus`'s per-frame loop (WordWeaverScene.js:262) — otherwise dozens of events per move. Coordinate with 5.6 so a *pick*-driven `focusOnDay` and a *bus*-driven one both emit consistently (and don't loop: a `navigateTo` → `focusOnDay` → `dayFocused` must not re-trigger `navigateTo`).
- **Edge cases:** Feedback loop — if something maps `dayFocused` back to `navigateTo`, guard against re-entrancy (emit `dayFocused` as a *result*, treat `navigateTo` as a *command*; don't echo); the §13.2 catalog still lacks `modeChanged`/`preferenceChanged` (the Phase-3/5.1 carry-forward) — note that `navigateTo`/`dayFocused`/`monthFocused` **are** in the catalog so this wiring has no catalog gap (unlike those); week-level `navigateTo` has no 3D analog (the ring is year→month→day) — map to the containing month or the week's first day and document.
- **UI/UX considerations:** This is what makes 2D↔3D navigation coherent — clicking a day in the 2D month view and switching to 3D should land on that day; jumping to today in 3D should update the top bar's focused date. Without it the two modes are islands (the persistent Phase-4/5 finding).
- **Data flow notes:** Inbound: `navigateTo {level, ...}` → focus method → camera. Outbound: focus method → `dayFocused`/`monthFocused` → 2D views / top bar update `focusedDate`.
- **Testing notes:** `focusOnDay` emits one `dayFocused` with the right date; `focusOnMonth` emits one `monthFocused`; emitting `navigateTo {level:"day", date}` on the bus moves the 3D camera to that day; no per-frame event spam; no `navigateTo`↔`dayFocused` infinite loop.
- **Performance notes:** One event per navigation — negligible; the explicit guard against per-frame emission is the watch-item.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Identical bus behavior.
- **Integration points:** §13.2 line 1418/436/1101-1102/1278, 5.1.9 (canonical bus), 5.5.8 (emitting methods), 5.6 (pick→focus→emit), Phase-3 top bar (consumes `dayFocused`/`monthFocused`), Phase-4 2D views (emit `navigateTo`).
- **Status:** **Missing (recurring bus gap).** Focus sets `_yearLayout.focusedDay` only; emits **no** §13.2 events; scene listens only to the duplicate bus's `timelineUpdated`; no `navigateTo` subscription. **Next:** emit `dayFocused`/`monthFocused` on commit + subscribe `navigateTo` → focus, on the canonical bus, loop-guarded.

## 5.5.10 — Reduced-motion snap, far-plane/post-processing close-out, Escape/keyboard-nav handoff

- **Purpose:** Close Phase 5's camera thread: honor `prefers-reduced-motion` (snap transitions per §23.3 line 1664), confirm the **far-plane fix** (5.5.2 / 5.1.4) and the **§5.8 optional bloom** post-processing note, hand the **Escape / arrow-key navigation** (§5.7 line 621-624) to 5.6 while owning the *camera* half (Escape → overview), and disposition the **duplicate camera/input subsystems** — so the camera system is converged, accessible, and documented.
- **Dependencies:** all prior 5.5 tasks, 5.4.6 (the shared scene-level reduced-motion gate), 5.6 (owns the raycaster/arrow-focus/Escape *interaction*), 5.1.4 (far-plane); §23.3 line 1664, §5.7 line 621-624, §5.8 line 632, §16.1 line 1302.
- **Acceptance criteria:** Under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`, camera focus moves **snap immediately** (lerp factor → 1 for one frame, or jump straight to the endpoint — §23.3 line 1664), observed live via the same `matchMedia` listener as 5.4.6 (no reload); the far-plane fix from 5.5.2 is verified to resolve 5.1.4 (full ring visible at overview); the §5.8 line 632 optional `UnrealBloomPass` (threshold 0.8 / strength 0.3, emissive-only, **disabled on mobile**) is documented as a performance-gated post step that, if added, composes after the camera/render (out of scope to *build* here unless trivial — note the seam); **Escape** returns the camera month-overview → year-overview (§5.7 line 624 — the *camera* action 5.5 owns; the *focus-stepping* keyboard nav of §5.7 line 621-623 is 5.6's); the duplicate camera/input subsystems are dispositioned (canonical 3D camera from 5.5.1; wall `CameraController` scoped to `CalendarApp`; single fly-input path from 5.5.6) and documented.
- **Implementation notes:** Gate `_updateCameraFocus` (WordWeaverScene.js:262-269) on the scene reduced-motion flag: when reduced, set the camera/target directly to the endpoint and clear the move (snap). Reuse the 5.4.6 scene-level `prefers-reduced-motion` flag (live `matchMedia` change listener) rather than a second probe (`prefersReducedMotion()` in perfFlags.js:6 is available). Add `Escape → focusOverview` to the (single, 5.5.6) keyboard handler — but coordinate with 5.6 so Escape's "month-overview then year-overview" two-step (collapse one level per press) lives in one place. For bloom (§5.8 line 632): document where an `EffectComposer`/`UnrealBloomPass` would slot (after `renderer.render`, WordWeaverScene.js:747) and that it's mobile-disabled + perf-gated — implement only if cheap, else leave as a documented seam. Record the final disposition of the three camera/input subsystems.
- **Edge cases:** Reduced-motion snap must also stop fly-inertia jumps from feeling jarring (fly itself isn't a "transition" — only `focusOn*` moves snap; document that free-fly is unaffected); Escape during an in-flight move should snap-collapse a level cleanly; bloom, if added, must be gated off on mobile and under perf flags (it's expensive — §5.8 line 632); the §13.2 catalog's missing `modeChanged`/`preferenceChanged` (Phase-3/5.1 carry) is *not* this task's to fix — just note the camera doesn't depend on them.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Reduced-motion users get instant, vestibular-safe camera jumps (no sweeping moves); Escape as "back out one level" is a learned, expected affordance; subtle bloom (if enabled) makes emissive day-glows pop without a heavy cracked-reality look (§5.8's restrained aesthetic).
- **Data flow notes:** `matchMedia` → scene reduced-motion flag → focus-snap gate. Escape (keyboard) → `focusOverview`. Bloom (optional) → post-render composite.
- **Testing notes:** With reduced-motion on, `focusOnDay` jumps instantly (snapshot: target pose reached in one frame, no intermediate); toggling the OS preference live changes behavior without reload; the full ring is visible at overview (5.1.4 fixed); Escape steps camera month→year overview; if bloom is enabled it's off on mobile and behind a perf flag; the three camera subsystems have a documented disposition.
- **Performance notes:** Reduced-motion snap is a free win (skips the lerp); bloom is the one *costly* optional item — keep it gated and mobile-off (§5.8 line 632).
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Reduced-motion honored both; bloom **disabled on mobile**; Escape is desktop-keyboard (mobile uses the back/overview UI affordance).
- **Integration points:** §23.3 line 1664, §5.7 line 621-624, §5.8 line 632, §16.1 line 1302, 5.4.6 (shared reduced-motion gate), 5.1.4 (far-plane), 5.5.1/5.5.6 (subsystem/input disposition), 5.6 (arrow-nav + Escape interaction half).
- **Status:** **Open (accessibility + close-out).** No reduced-motion snap (`_updateCameraFocus` always eases); far-plane fix pending in 5.5.2; bloom absent (optional); Escape/arrow nav unhandled; three camera/input subsystems unreconciled. **Next:** snap under reduced motion (shared gate), verify the far-plane fix, document the optional bloom seam, add Escape→overview, disposition the subsystems.


---

<!-- source: phase-05-milestone-5.6-interaction.md -->

# Phase 5 — Milestone 5.6: Interaction Model (3D) (Expanded)

**Goal:** Specify the WordWeaver 3D interaction layer (§5.7): a `Raycaster` driven from the pointer that tests **`DayBlock3D` meshes (and month labels)**, acting on the **first intersection** — producing **hover** (block emissive increase + Y translation, pointer cursor, a tooltip showing day number + event count), **click/tap** (focus the block via `focusOnDay`; a second click on an already-focused day **opens the day detail panel**), **month-label click** (`focusOnMonth`), **non-interactive empty/decorative surfaces** (padding days and `AtomGlyph3D` atoms are ignored), and **keyboard navigation** (arrows move focus between adjacent blocks, Enter focuses, Escape steps month-overview → year-overview). This milestone is grounded against the **already-existing** pick path inside [`WordWeaverScene.js`](../../src/wordweaver/WordWeaverScene.js) (`_raycaster`/`_pointer`, `_pointerToNdc`, `_collectClickTargets`, `_pick`, `_handlePointerMove`, `_handlePointerDown`, the `pointermove`/`pointerdown` listeners) and the [`DayBlock3D.js`](../../src/wordweaver/DayBlock3D.js) state hooks (`setProximity`, `userData.dayBlock`, `group.name`) — so every task reconciles built / gap / next-step, not greenfield. It owns the **pick → hover/click → focus/panel routing**; it consumes 5.4.8 (atom raycast-exclusion contract), 5.5.8 (the day-panel trigger rule), and 5.5.9 (the §13.2 bus emit/consume); it does **not** own the camera move itself (5.5), the day tile visuals (5.3), the ring layout (5.2), or the decorations (5.4).

**Spec alignment:** §5.7 line 604-624 (Raycaster updated **each animation frame**, tests **all `DayBlock3D` meshes**, only the **first** intersection acted upon, lines 606-607; hover = emissive increase + Y translation, pointer cursor, **tooltip day-number + event-count near cursor**, lines 609-610; click/tap → focused `DayBlock3D` triggers `focusOnDay()`, **second click on an already-focused day opens the day detail panel**, lines 612-613; **month-label click → `focusOnMonth()`**, lines 615-616; **empty/out-of-month day blocks non-interactive**, invisible/near-transparent, **raycaster ignores them**, lines 618-619; **keyboard nav** — arrows move focus between adjacent blocks, Enter focuses the keyboard-focused day, **Escape returns camera month-overview → year-overview**, lines 621-624), §16.1 line 1304 (frame-loop step 3 = "Raycast from camera through pointer; update hover state"), §13.2 line 1418 (3D navigation updates `focusedDate`, emits `dayFocused`/`monthFocused`), §13.2 line 1101-1102 (pick exemplar: click day → `focusOnDay(3,14)` → camera lerps), §5.5 line 562 (atoms have **no interactive function** — the 5.4.8 exclusion this milestone enforces in the raycaster), §1.4 line 88 (interaction must stay responsive — raycast can't blow the frame budget), §27 (mobile touch ergonomics — tap vs orbit-drag).

**Sequencing:** **Sixth and final milestone of Phase 5.** It depends on 5.1 (the canonical scene that owns the pointer listeners and the per-frame loop), 5.2 (the layout supplies `dayBlocks`/`monthClusters`/`findDayBlock` and the grid adjacency keyboard nav walks), 5.3 (the `DayBlock3D` hover/focus state hooks — and the 5.3.5 finding that the block has only a **binary camera-proximity** state, not a 3-state rest/hover/focus, which this milestone's raycast-hover completes), 5.4.8 (the atom non-interactive contract this raycaster honors), and 5.5 (the focus API + day-panel rule + bus wiring this routing **calls into** — 5.6 turns a pick into a 5.5 `focusOn*` call). It carries the two Phase-5 recurring threads: the **duplicate / parallel-stack** pattern — here **two pick systems** coexist (`_collectClickTargets` mixes the **year-ring `DayBlock3D`** meshes **and** the satellite-timeline `_pickables` nodes, WordWeaverScene.js:645-657) — and the **§13.2 event-bus gap** (clicks route through a constructor `onNodeClick` callback, not the Phase-2 bus — 5.6.10, consuming 5.5.9).

---

## Current implementation status (reconciliation)

A working pick path **already exists and is live in the year ring** — a `Raycaster`, pointer→NDC conversion, a target collector, a parent-walk to resolve the hit `DayBlock3D`, a hover cursor, and a click that calls `focusOnDay`. But it is **event-driven, not per-frame**; hover sets **only the cursor** (no block emissive/Y state, no tooltip); click **immediately** focuses **and** fires a callback with a **fabricated note** (no second-click-opens-panel two-step); **month labels aren't pickable** (so `focusOnMonth` is never reached from interaction); there's **no tap-vs-orbit-drag discrimination** (orbiting from a block focuses unintentionally); there's **no keyboard navigation**; the target list is **rebuilt every pointer event** over ~365×3 meshes (perf); it routes through a constructor **callback, not the §13.2 bus**; and it mixes **two pick systems**. This is an upgrade/reconcile + complete milestone, not greenfield.

- **Two pick systems mixed in one collector.** `_collectClickTargets` (WordWeaverScene.js:645-657) gathers **both** the satellite-timeline `_pickables` node meshes (traversed) **and** every year-ring `DayBlock3D`'s `panel`/`noteSurface`/`eventGlow` — and `_pick` (:659-672) resolves either a `userData.dayBlock` **or** a `userData.node`. In **3D year mode** the spec (§5.7 line 607) wants the raycaster to test **`DayBlock3D` meshes** (and labels, line 615); the timeline-node pick is a *different* subsystem (the satellite weave window). 5.6.1 scopes the year-ring pick to day blocks + month labels and reconciles/segregates the timeline-node path.
- **Raycast is event-driven (pointermove/pointerdown), not per-frame.** §5.7 line 607 / §16.1 line 1304 describe a raycaster **updated each animation frame** (frame-loop step 3). Live binds `pointermove → _handlePointerMove` and `pointerdown → _handlePointerDown` (WordWeaverScene.js:137-138) and raycasts **inside those events** — there is **no** per-frame raycast in `_tick` (WordWeaverScene.js:711-732). Event-driven is arguably *cheaper* (no idle raycasts) and is a legitimate design — but it diverges from the spec's per-frame model and means hover doesn't update when the **camera** moves under a stationary pointer (e.g. during a focus lerp or fly). 5.6.2 reconciles cadence (keep event-driven + a camera-moved re-pick, or move to throttled per-frame) and **caches the target list**.
- **The target list is rebuilt on every pointer event over hundreds of meshes.** `_collectClickTargets` (WordWeaverScene.js:645-657) allocates a fresh array and **traverses** every `_pickables` group plus pushes 3 meshes per day block (~365 days × 3 ≈ 1,100 meshes) **on every `pointermove`** (which fires continuously). That is a per-move allocation + traversal + a full `intersectObjects` over ~1,100 objects — the §1.4 line 88 / §16 responsiveness risk. 5.6.2 caches a flattened pickable array (rebuilt only on layout change) and considers bounding-volume/`Layers` narrowing.
- **Hover sets only the cursor — no block hover state, no tooltip.** §5.7 line 609-610 wants the intersected block to enter a **hover state (emissive increase + Y translation)**, the cursor to become a pointer, **and a tooltip** (day number + event count) near the cursor. Live `_handlePointerMove` (WordWeaverScene.js:674-682) only tracks `_hovered` (a dateIso string) and toggles `canvas.style.cursor` between `"pointer"`/`"grab"` — it does **not** call any hover method on the `DayBlock3D` and shows **no tooltip**. The block's only state hook is `setProximity(near)` (DayBlock3D.js:378-385), which is **camera-distance-driven** (set in `layout.update` at `dist < 4.2`, WordWeaverScene.js:1031-1032), **not** raycast-hover — the 5.3.5 finding (binary proximity, not 3-state rest/hover/focus). 5.6.3 adds a raycast-driven hover state distinct from proximity; 5.6.4 adds the tooltip.
- **Click immediately focuses *and* fires a fabricated callback — no second-click-opens-panel two-step.** §5.7 line 612-613: a click triggers `focusOnDay()`, and **if the day is already focused, a second click opens the day detail panel**. Live `_handlePointerDown` (WordWeaverScene.js:684-698) calls `focusOnDay(...)` **and** immediately `onNodeClick({date, time:"12:00", text:"Day N", node})` with a **fabricated note** (`time:"12:00"`, `text:"Day {day}"`) on the **first** interaction — there is no "already-focused → open panel" gate, and the fake note is a code smell (it invents data the model doesn't have). It also fires on **`pointerdown`** (not a click/tap-up), so it can't distinguish a tap from the start of an orbit drag (5.6.6). 5.6.5 implements the focus-then-second-click-panel rule (reconciled with 5.5.8) and removes the fabricated note.
- **No tap-vs-orbit-drag discrimination.** Because the action fires on `pointerdown` (WordWeaverScene.js:684), **any** press that lands on a block focuses it — including the press that *begins an OrbitControls drag* to orbit the scene. The user can't rotate the view starting from a day block without triggering a focus. §5.7 line 612 ("on click/tap") and §27 (touch) imply a true click/tap (down→up with little movement). 5.6.6 adds a movement-threshold + down/up pairing so dragging orbits and only a stationary tap focuses.
- **Month labels aren't pickable — `focusOnMonth` is never reached from interaction.** §5.7 line 615-616: clicking a **month label** triggers `focusOnMonth()`. Live month labels (`createMonthLabelMesh` → `label.mesh`, WordWeaverScene.js:898-900) are added to the cluster but carry **no pick `userData`** and are **not** included in `_collectClickTargets` (which only gathers `_pickables` + day-block meshes) — so a label click hits nothing, and `focusOnMonth` (WordWeaverScene.js:214, which *exists*) is **never** invoked by the pointer. 5.6.7 makes labels pickable and routes them to `focusOnMonth`.
- **Empty/out-of-month days: the 3D ring currently has none — but 5.2.4 may add them, and they must stay non-interactive.** §5.7 line 618-619 says empty/out-of-month day blocks are non-interactive and the raycaster ignores them. Live `populateMonthCluster` creates `DayBlock3D` **only** for `day = 1..daysInMonth` (WordWeaverScene.js:902-936) — there are **no leading/trailing padding blocks** (the 5.2.4 finding: "NO leading/trailing dimmed empty cells per line 511"). So today there's nothing to ignore — but **if 5.2.4 adds dimmed padding cells**, they must be excluded from the pick set (and `AtomGlyph3D` atoms must be excluded per 5.4.8). 5.6.8 makes the non-interactive contract explicit for both.
- **No keyboard navigation (arrows / Enter / Escape).** §5.7 line 621-624 wants arrow keys to move focus between adjacent day blocks, Enter to focus the keyboard-focused day, and **Escape to step camera month-overview → year-overview**. The only keyboard handling in 3D is the **fly movement** (WASD/Q-E, 5.5.6) in `WordWeaverTimelineViewport`/`WordWeaverChrome` — there is **no** day-focus keyboard nav and **no** Escape-to-overview. 5.6.9 adds grid-adjacency arrow nav + Enter; Escape's camera half is coordinated with 5.5.10.
- **Routes through a constructor `onNodeClick` callback, not the §13.2 bus.** Clicks call `this.onNodeClick({...})` (WordWeaverScene.js:691-708) — a constructor-injected callback — **not** the Phase-2 `src/utils/EventBus.js`. So a pick emits **no** `dayFocused`/`monthFocused` and opening the day panel is wired through the callback chain, not the §13.2 contract (the recurring Phase-4/5 bus gap, and the 5.5.9 wiring this should consume). 5.6.10 routes interaction through the canonical bus.
- **What's already right (keep):** a single `Raycaster` + reused `_pointer` `Vector2` (no per-event allocation of those) ✓ (WordWeaverScene.js:114-115); correct **pointer→NDC** math accounting for canvas rect (`_pointerToNdc`, :639-642) ✓; **first-intersection** semantics (`hits[0]`, :664) ✓ (§5.7 line 607); robust **parent-walk** to resolve the owning `DayBlock3D` via `userData.dayBlock` (:664-669) — works regardless of which child mesh is hit ✓; stable pick handle (`group.name = "day-block-{iso}"` + `userData.dayBlock`, DayBlock3D.js:224-225) ✓; **Pointer Events** (unified mouse+touch) ✓; `preventDefault`/`stopPropagation` on a real hit so the canvas doesn't also pan ✓ (:688-689); pointer listeners are **removed on dispose** (WordWeaverScene.js:761-762) ✓; hover **cursor** feedback exists ✓ (:680); atoms are **already not** in the pick set (added to clusters but not `_pickables`/`dayBlocks`) — so 5.4.8 is currently satisfied *implicitly* (5.6.8 makes it explicit) ✓.

---

## 5.6.1 — Canonical raycaster/pick scope & two-pick-system reconciliation

- **Purpose:** Establish one canonical pick path for the **3D year ring** that tests **`DayBlock3D` meshes + month labels** (§5.7 line 607/615), and reconcile it with the **second** pick system the same collector mixes in — the satellite-timeline `_pickables` nodes — so the year-ring interaction has a single, scoped, well-defined hit set.
- **Dependencies:** 5.1 (the scene owns the raycaster/listeners), 5.2 (`dayBlocks`/`monthClusters` supply the targets), 5.4.8 (atom exclusion), 5.5 (the focus API the pick calls); §5.7 line 606-607/615, §11 (subsystem boundaries).
- **Acceptance criteria:** In 3D year mode the raycaster's target set is **exactly** the interactive `DayBlock3D` meshes **plus** the month-label meshes (5.6.7) — **not** the satellite-timeline `_pickables` nodes (those belong to the weave window, a separate subsystem); `_pick`'s resolution still parent-walks to `userData.dayBlock` (kept) and adds a `userData.monthLabel` branch (5.6.7); the timeline-node pick path is either segregated behind a mode check (only active in the satellite/weave context) or documented as a separate collector — the two are not conflated in the year-ring path; atoms are excluded (5.6.8 / 5.4.8).
- **Implementation notes:** Split `_collectClickTargets` (WordWeaverScene.js:645-657): in year/3D mode return day-block interactive meshes + label meshes; gate the `_pickables` timeline-node traversal to the context where it's live (it currently runs unconditionally). Keep `_pick`'s parent-walk (:664-669) and extend it to recognize labels (5.6.7). Confirm via the import/usage graph which mode populates `_pickables` (the satellite timeline) vs `dayBlocks` (the year ring) and don't cross them.
- **Edge cases:** If both subsystems can be mounted at once (embed timeline + year ring), the collector must pick the right set per active mode — don't raycast weave nodes while in the year ring; a day block's three meshes (`panel`/`noteSurface`/`eventGlow`) all resolve to the same `dayBlock` via the parent-walk — dedupe is unnecessary (first hit wins) but the target list shouldn't double-count for perf (5.6.2).
- **UI/UX considerations:** A scoped hit set makes hover/click predictable (only days and labels respond), matching the spec's interaction model; mixing in weave nodes would make stray objects pickable in the year ring.
- **Data flow notes:** Active mode → target set → raycast → resolved `dayBlock`/`monthLabel` → 5.5 focus call.
- **Testing notes:** In year mode, raycasting returns only day blocks or month labels (never a weave node); the timeline-node path is inert in the year ring; the parent-walk resolves any of a block's 3 meshes to the one `dayBlock`.
- **Performance notes:** Scoping the set (no weave-node traversal in year mode) shrinks the raycast set — a direct 5.6.2 win.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Same scope; input differs (5.6.6 tap, 5.6.9 keyboard desktop-only).
- **Integration points:** §5.7 line 607/615, 5.2 (targets), 5.4.8/5.6.8 (atom exclusion), 5.6.7 (labels), 5.6.2 (caching), 5.5 (focus).
- **Status:** **Mixed (two pick systems).** `_collectClickTargets` gathers year-ring day blocks **and** satellite-timeline `_pickables` nodes in one set. **Next:** scope the year-ring pick to day blocks + labels, segregate/gate the timeline-node path, keep the parent-walk.

## 5.6.2 — Raycast cadence (per-frame vs event) & cached pickable targets

- **Purpose:** Reconcile the raycast cadence to §5.7 line 607 / §16.1 line 1304 (raycaster "updated each animation frame", frame-loop step 3) versus the live **event-driven** raycast, and eliminate the **per-event rebuild** of a ~1,100-mesh target list — so hover stays correct when the camera moves and the pick cost stays within the §1.4 frame budget.
- **Dependencies:** 5.6.1 (the scoped target set to cache), 5.6.3 (hover consumes the result), 5.1 (the frame loop / `_tick`), 5.5.4 (camera moves that should re-pick); §5.7 line 607, §16.1 line 1304, §1.4 line 88.
- **Acceptance criteria:** The pickable target array is **cached** (built once per layout change — when `dayBlocks`/labels are (re)created in 5.2's build/rebuild — not per pointer event); the raycast either (a) runs **per-frame** in `_tick` against the cached set (spec-literal, frame-loop step 3) **or** (b) stays **event-driven on pointermove** *plus* re-picks when the **camera moves** under a stationary pointer (so a focus lerp/fly updates hover) — pick one and document; hover updates correctly while the camera animates; no per-event array allocation/traversal of the full block set remains; the per-frame/per-move raycast cost is bounded (measured within budget at full ring).
- **Implementation notes:** Replace the per-call `_collectClickTargets` allocation (WordWeaverScene.js:645-657) with a cached `this._pickTargets` rebuilt in the layout build/rebuild path (5.2.8) and invalidated on rebuild; if going per-frame, move the `setFromCamera`+`intersectObjects` into `_tick` (gated to `getCalendarMode()==="3d"`, WordWeaverScene.js:729) using the last known `_pointer`; if staying event-driven, add a re-pick when `_cameraFocus`/fly changed the camera since last pointer move. Consider `Layers` (put interactive day blocks on a dedicated layer and set `raycaster.layers`) or per-cluster bounding-sphere pre-cull to shrink the test set further.
- **Edge cases:** A per-frame raycast every frame even when idle is wasted work — throttle to "pointer or camera changed since last frame"; the cached target set must invalidate on layout rebuild (5.2.8 full rebuild) or stale meshes get tested; pointer leaving the canvas must clear hover (no listener fires inside, so add a `pointerleave` clear, ties to 5.6.3); during a focus lerp the hovered block under the cursor changes — the camera-moved re-pick covers it.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Hover that lags or sticks (stale during camera motion) feels broken; the cached/throttled approach keeps it crisp without burning frames.
- **Data flow notes:** Layout build → cached targets; pointer/camera change → raycast → hover/click resolution.
- **Testing notes:** Target array is built once per layout (instrument the builder), not per pointer event; hover updates while the camera animates over a still pointer; `renderer`/profiler shows no per-move allocation spike; idle frames do no raycast.
- **Performance notes:** This is the headline interaction-perf fix — removes ~1,100-mesh allocation+traversal per `pointermove` and bounds the raycast via caching/layers (§1.4 line 88).
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Mobile has no hover (5.6.3) — the per-move raycast there is only for tap resolution; desktop benefits most from caching during continuous hover.
- **Integration points:** §5.7 line 607, §16.1 line 1304, 5.6.1 (set), 5.6.3 (hover), 5.2.8 (rebuild invalidation), 5.1 (`_tick`).
- **Status:** **Divergent (cadence) + costly (rebuild).** Raycast is event-driven (not per-frame) and rebuilds a ~1,100-mesh target list every `pointermove`. **Next:** cache the target set (invalidate on rebuild), choose per-frame or event+camera-moved re-pick, narrow via layers/bounds.

## 5.6.3 — Raycast-driven hover state on `DayBlock3D` (emissive + Y translation)

- **Purpose:** Implement the §5.7 line 609 hover state — the intersected `DayBlock3D` gets an **emissive increase + slight Y translation** and the cursor becomes a pointer — as a **raycast-driven** state **distinct** from the existing camera-proximity (`setProximity`), completing the 5.3.5 rest/hover/focus model.
- **Dependencies:** 5.6.2 (the hover hit each frame/move), 5.3.5 (the 3-state model — `setProximity` is binary; this adds the hover tier), 5.3 (`DayBlock3D` material/transform hooks); §5.7 line 609, §16.1 line 1304 ("update hover state").
- **Acceptance criteria:** When the raycaster intersects a `DayBlock3D`, that block enters a **hover** visual state (emissive bump + small +Y lift, eased — not a snap) and the cursor is `pointer`; when the pointer leaves it (or the canvas), the block returns to its rest/proximity state; hover is **separate** from `setProximity` (camera-distance) — a block can be proximity-near *and* hovered, and the states compose sensibly (per 5.3.5's rest/hover/focus precedence); only **one** block is hovered at a time (the first intersection); under reduced motion the Y-translation is omitted/snapped (5.6.10).
- **Implementation notes:** Add a `setHover(on)` to `DayBlock3D` (alongside `setProximity`, DayBlock3D.js:378-385) that adjusts `edgeMat.emissiveIntensity` and a target +Y offset, eased in the block's `update` (the existing lerp at DayBlock3D.js:392-399 is the pattern); in `_handlePointerMove`/the per-frame pick (5.6.2), when `_hovered` changes, call `setHover(false)` on the old block and `setHover(true)` on the new (resolve the block from `userData.dayBlock`, not just the dateIso string the live code stores). Keep the cursor toggle (WordWeaverScene.js:680). Add a `pointerleave` handler to clear hover (5.6.2 edge case).
- **Edge cases:** Hover + focus on the same block (the focused day is also under the pointer) must not double-lift or fight `setProximity` — define precedence (focus > hover > proximity > rest, per 5.3.5); rapid pointer moves across many blocks must not leave a stuck-hovered block (always clear the previous); touch has no hover — `setHover` shouldn't fire on mobile (5.6.4 handles the no-hover platform); the Y-lift must not push a block into its neighbor or the camera near-plane.
- **UI/UX considerations:** A subtle emissive+lift makes the hovered day feel responsive and "pickable" before the click — the core affordance the cursor alone (live) doesn't convey; eased, not snapped, keeps it calm (§1.4).
- **Data flow notes:** Raycast hit (5.6.2) → `_hovered` block → `setHover` → eased visual in `update`.
- **Testing notes:** Hovering a day raises its emissive + lifts it slightly; moving off restores it; only one block hovered at once; hover and proximity/focus compose per the precedence; reduced motion drops the lift.
- **Performance notes:** One `setHover` toggle per hover change (not per frame) — negligible; the eased update is already in the block's per-frame `update`.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Desktop only (touch has no hover); mobile gets tap feedback via 5.6.5, not hover.
- **Integration points:** §5.7 line 609, §16.1 line 1304, 5.3.5 (3-state model), 5.6.2 (hit source), 5.6.4 (tooltip pairs with hover), 5.6.10 (reduced-motion).
- **Status:** **Missing (cursor only).** Live sets only `canvas.style.cursor`; no block hover state; `setProximity` is camera-distance, not raycast-hover. **Next:** add `setHover` (emissive + eased +Y), drive it from the pick, clear on leave, compose with proximity/focus per 5.3.5.

## 5.6.4 — Hover tooltip (day number + event count)

- **Purpose:** Implement the §5.7 line 610 tooltip — when hovering a `DayBlock3D`, a small tooltip showing the **day number and event count** appears **near the cursor** — giving a quick read before committing to a focus/click.
- **Dependencies:** 5.6.3 (hover state drives tooltip show/hide), 5.3 (`DayBlock3D` exposes day + event count — the 5.3.9 tooltip-data accessor), 5.2 (the block's date); §5.7 line 610, §27 (mobile no-hover).
- **Acceptance criteria:** On hover, a DOM tooltip (overlaid on the canvas) shows the **day number** and **event count** (e.g. "14 · 3 events" / "14 · No events"); it is positioned near the cursor and follows it (or anchors to the block's projected screen position); it hides on hover-out / pointer-leave; it reads its data from the block (5.3.9 accessor — `day`, event count from the block's events), **not** from a fabricated string; on **mobile** (no hover) the tooltip is suppressed (tap goes straight to focus, 5.6.5) or shown briefly on tap; it's accessible (not focus-trapping; `aria` live-region optional) and doesn't intercept pointer events (pointer-events: none).
- **Implementation notes:** Add a lightweight DOM tooltip element to the scene's mount (reuse the embed/chrome overlay layer); on `_hovered` change (5.6.3) populate it from the hovered `DayBlock3D` (add a `getTooltipData()` to DayBlock3D returning `{ day, eventCount }`, the 5.3.9 accessor) and position it from the pointer (clientX/Y) or the block's projected NDC→screen point; set `pointer-events: none` so it never steals the next hover/click; hide on `pointerleave`/hover-clear. Avoid per-frame DOM writes — update only on hover change + on move if following the cursor (throttle).
- **Edge cases:** Tooltip near the canvas edge must clamp on-screen (don't overflow); fast hover changes must not flicker (debounce show/hide slightly); event count must match what the block actually renders (consistent with 5.3's summary/+N logic); mobile long-press could optionally show it, but the default is tap-to-focus (don't block the tap); reduced motion doesn't affect the tooltip (it's not motion).
- **UI/UX considerations:** A quick "day + count" peek lets users scan the ring without focusing each day — a key affordance for the year-at-a-glance model; it must be unobtrusive and never occlude the block it describes.
- **Data flow notes:** Hovered block → `getTooltipData()` → tooltip DOM text + position.
- **Testing notes:** Hover shows "{day} · {count}"; it follows/anchors correctly and clamps at edges; hides on leave; reads real block data (no fabricated note); suppressed/var on mobile; `pointer-events:none` (hovering the tooltip doesn't change hover).
- **Performance notes:** DOM tooltip updated only on hover change (+ throttled follow) — negligible; far cheaper than a 3D text label.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Desktop hover tooltip; mobile suppresses or briefly shows on tap.
- **Integration points:** §5.7 line 610, 5.6.3 (hover), 5.3.9 (tooltip-data accessor), 5.2 (date), §27 (mobile).
- **Status:** **Missing.** No tooltip; hover shows only a cursor; the click path even fabricates a `"Day N"` note instead of reading real data. **Next:** DOM tooltip (day + event count) from a `getTooltipData()` accessor, cursor-anchored, edge-clamped, `pointer-events:none`, mobile-suppressed.

## 5.6.5 — Click/tap → `focusOnDay`; second click on focused day → open day panel

- **Purpose:** Reconcile the click/tap behavior to §5.7 line 612-613 — a click/tap focuses the day via `focusOnDay()`, and a **second** click on the **already-focused** day **opens the day detail panel** — replacing the live single-`pointerdown` path that **immediately** focuses **and** fires `onNodeClick` with a **fabricated note**, and aligning with the day-panel-trigger rule settled in 5.5.8.
- **Dependencies:** 5.5.8 (the focus API + the settled panel-trigger rule), 5.6.6 (tap-vs-drag, so this fires on a real tap), 5.5.9 (bus emit on focus), 5.3 (the day panel content); §5.7 line 612-613, §13.2 line 1101-1102.
- **Acceptance criteria:** A click/tap on an **unfocused** day calls `focusOnDay(monthIndex, day)` (camera moves, 5.5), sets it as the focused day, and emits `dayFocused` (5.5.9) — but does **not** open the panel; a **second** click/tap on the **already-focused** day **opens the day detail panel** (the real panel, populated from the model — not a fabricated `{time:"12:00", text:"Day N"}`); the fabricated-note `onNodeClick` payload is removed/replaced with a real day reference (date) routed via the bus (5.6.10); the rule matches 5.5.8's decision (if 5.5.8 chose "focus opens panel immediately" instead, this conforms to that single rule — the two milestones must not disagree); it fires on a **tap/click** (down→up, 5.6.6), not a raw `pointerdown`.
- **Implementation notes:** In the click handler (rework `_handlePointerDown`, WordWeaverScene.js:684-698): resolve the `dayBlock`; if it's **not** the current `_yearLayout.focusedDay`, call `focusOnDay` (which already sets `focusedDay` + proximity, WordWeaverScene.js:226-234) and emit `dayFocused`; if it **is** already focused, open the day panel (via the bus/5.6.10, populated from the model for that date). **Delete** the fabricated `onNodeClick({time:"12:00", text:"Day {day}"})` (WordWeaverScene.js:691-696) — pass only the date/day reference. Gate the whole thing behind the tap discrimination (5.6.6) so an orbit-drag from a block doesn't focus.
- **Edge cases:** Clicking a different day while one is focused = focus the new one (not open panel) — only the **same already-focused** day opens the panel; the panel must read **real** events for that date (consistent with the day-detail spec); double-tap timing on mobile vs the "second tap" semantics — the rule is "tap a day that is *currently focused*", not a timed double-tap (so it works even minutes later); if 5.5.8 chose immediate-panel, there is no second-click step (conform); Escape/closing the panel doesn't unfocus the day (5.6.9).
- **UI/UX considerations:** Focus-then-open is a gentle two-step (look, then commit) that avoids yanking open a panel on every glance-click; it must feel consistent with the same gesture on 2D and with whatever 5.5.8 settled.
- **Data flow notes:** Tap → resolve block → (unfocused) `focusOnDay` + `dayFocused`; (focused again) open day panel (bus) ← model events for the date.
- **Testing notes:** First tap focuses (camera moves, no panel); second tap on the same focused day opens the panel with real events; tapping another day focuses it instead; no fabricated note appears anywhere; tap (not drag) is required (5.6.6).
- **Performance notes:** Negligible; one focus call or one panel open per tap.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Same rule; tap on mobile == click on desktop (Pointer Events unify).
- **Integration points:** §5.7 line 612-613, 5.5.8 (panel rule), 5.5.9 (bus emit), 5.6.6 (tap gate), 5.6.10 (panel via bus), 5.3 (panel content).
- **Status:** **Divergent.** Live focuses **and** fires a fabricated-note callback on first `pointerdown` — no second-click-opens-panel two-step, no real day data. **Next:** first tap → `focusOnDay`+`dayFocused`; second tap on the focused day → open the real day panel; remove the fabricated note; gate behind tap discrimination.

## 5.6.6 — Tap vs orbit-drag discrimination (down/up + movement threshold)

- **Purpose:** Distinguish a **tap/click** (focus intent) from the **start of an orbit/pan drag** (camera intent), so a user can rotate the scene starting on a day block without triggering `focusOnDay` — fixing the live behavior where the action fires on raw `pointerdown` (WordWeaverScene.js:684) and any press on a block focuses it.
- **Dependencies:** 5.6.5 (the click action being gated), 5.5.5/5.5.7 (OrbitControls/touch own the drag), 5.6.2 (the pick at down/up); §5.7 line 612 ("on click/tap"), §27 (touch drag).
- **Acceptance criteria:** The focus/panel action fires only when `pointerdown` and `pointerup` land on the **same** block with **little movement** between them (below a small px threshold) within a short time — i.e. a genuine click/tap; a press-and-drag (movement beyond the threshold) is treated as an **orbit/pan** and triggers **no** focus; this works for both **mouse** and **touch** (Pointer Events); it doesn't break OrbitControls' own drag handling (the canvas still orbits/pans); `preventDefault`/`stopPropagation` is applied only when committing a tap on a real hit (not on drags, so OrbitControls still gets the drag).
- **Implementation notes:** Replace the immediate-action `_handlePointerDown` with a down/up pair: on `pointerdown` record the pointer position, the pick, and time (don't act); on `pointerup` (or `pointermove` exceeding the threshold → cancel), if movement < threshold (e.g. ~5px) and same block, fire the 5.6.5 action; otherwise treat as a drag and do nothing. Be careful **not** to `preventDefault` on the down (that would block OrbitControls' drag) — only consume the event when committing a tap. Add `pointercancel` handling (touch interruptions).
- **Edge cases:** A tiny jitter during a tap (especially touch) must stay under the threshold — tune for touch (slightly larger threshold); a drag that starts on a block but releases over another block = orbit, not focus; multi-touch (pinch-zoom, 5.5.7) must never register as a tap; releasing outside the canvas = cancel; the threshold in px should account for devicePixelRatio.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Being able to orbit from anywhere — including over a day — is essential for a free-look 3D space; the current "any press focuses" makes the scene feel like it grabs you.
- **Data flow notes:** down (record) → move (maybe cancel) → up (commit if tap) → 5.6.5 action.
- **Testing notes:** Click-without-moving focuses; press-drag from a block orbits and does **not** focus; touch tap focuses, touch drag orbits; pinch never focuses; release-outside cancels; OrbitControls drag still works from over a block.
- **Performance notes:** Negligible (a couple of stored coords); removes spurious focus calls during orbiting.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Both; touch uses a slightly larger movement threshold.
- **Integration points:** §5.7 line 612, §27, 5.6.5 (gated action), 5.5.5/5.5.7 (drag owners), 5.6.2 (pick).
- **Status:** **Missing.** Action fires on raw `pointerdown` — any press on a block focuses, so orbiting from a day is impossible. **Next:** down/up + movement-threshold tap discrimination (mouse+touch), don't `preventDefault` drags, handle `pointercancel`.

## 5.6.7 — Month-label click → `focusOnMonth`

- **Purpose:** Wire the §5.7 line 615-616 behavior — clicking a **month label** triggers `focusOnMonth()` (camera moves to view that cluster, no panel) — by making the label meshes pickable and routing them through the pick path (today labels aren't in the target set, so `focusOnMonth`, which exists, is never reached from interaction).
- **Dependencies:** 5.6.1 (the scoped target set includes labels), 5.6.2 (cached targets include labels), 5.5 (`focusOnMonth` + bus), 5.2.5 (the label mesh — billboarded, the 5.2.5 finding); §5.7 line 615-616, §13.2 line 1278 (`focusOnMonth(5)` exemplar).
- **Acceptance criteria:** Each month label mesh carries a pick handle (`userData.monthLabel = { monthIndex }` or `userData.monthIndex`) and is included in the raycast target set (5.6.1/5.6.2); `_pick` resolves a label hit to its `monthIndex`; a click/tap (5.6.6) on a label calls `focusOnMonth(monthIndex)` (WordWeaverScene.js:214) and emits `monthFocused` (5.5.9); **no** day panel opens on a label click (§5.7 line 616 — month focus only); hover on a label gives pointer-cursor feedback (and optionally a subtle label highlight) but no day-tooltip.
- **Implementation notes:** In `populateMonthCluster` where the label is created (WordWeaverScene.js:898-900), set `label.mesh.userData.monthLabel = { monthIndex }` (and a stable `name`); include label meshes in the 5.6.1 scoped collector; extend `_pick`'s parent-walk (WordWeaverScene.js:664-669) to return `{ monthLabel }` when it encounters `userData.monthLabel`; in the click handler (5.6.5/5.6.6) branch on `monthLabel` → `focusOnMonth(monthIndex)` + `monthFocused`. Keep labels billboarded (5.2.5) so they're always face-on to raycast.
- **Edge cases:** A label overlapping a day block (depth) — raycast first-hit should resolve correctly (label is in front at `z=0.2`, WordWeaverScene.js:899); the label texture is a `Sprite`/plane — ensure its geometry is actually hit-testable (sprites raycast by their quad); month focus should not steal focus from a currently-focused day's panel state unexpectedly (define: month-label click clears day focus → month overview); reduced motion snaps the month move (5.5.10).
- **UI/UX considerations:** Clicking the month name to "zoom to this month" is an intuitive, discoverable affordance; without it `focusOnMonth` is dead code reachable only programmatically.
- **Data flow notes:** Label hit → `monthIndex` → `focusOnMonth` → camera move + `monthFocused` (bus).
- **Testing notes:** Clicking a month label moves the camera to that cluster (~20 units, 5.5.3) and emits `monthFocused`; no day panel opens; label hover shows pointer cursor; the label resolves to the correct `monthIndex`.
- **Performance notes:** +12 label meshes in the target set — negligible.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Same (tap a label = focus the month).
- **Integration points:** §5.7 line 615-616, §13.2 line 1278, 5.6.1/5.6.2 (targets), 5.5 (`focusOnMonth`/bus), 5.2.5 (label billboarding).
- **Status:** **Missing (labels not pickable).** `focusOnMonth` exists but month labels carry no pick handle and aren't in the target set, so interaction never calls it. **Next:** tag labels with `monthIndex`, include in the pick set, resolve in `_pick`, route click → `focusOnMonth` + `monthFocused`.

## 5.6.8 — Non-interactive surfaces: padding days & atom exclusion (consume 5.4.8)

- **Purpose:** Enforce the §5.7 line 618-619 non-interactive contract (empty/out-of-month day blocks are ignored by the raycaster) **and** the §5.5 line 562 / 5.4.8 atom-exclusion contract — so only **real** day blocks and month labels are pickable, and decorations/padding never intercept hover or clicks.
- **Dependencies:** 5.4.8 (the atom non-interactive contract this consumes), 5.2.4 (if/when dimmed padding cells are added — they must be non-interactive), 5.6.1 (the scoped target set); §5.7 line 618-619, §5.5 line 562.
- **Acceptance criteria:** `AtomGlyph3D` atoms are **excluded** from the raycast (per 5.4.8 — via the agreed mechanism: `raycast = () => {}`, a non-interactive `Layer`, or a `userData.nonInteractive` filter) — a ray through an atom in front of a day still selects the **day**, never the atom; **if** 5.2.4 adds leading/trailing **padding** day cells, they are marked non-interactive (excluded from the target set / `userData.nonInteractive`) and the raycaster ignores them (no hover, no focus); the halo planes, month-cluster backing, and §5.8 background are likewise not pickable; the contract is the **consumer side** of 5.4.8 (both sides agree on the mechanism).
- **Implementation notes:** Confirm atoms stay out of the year-ring target set (they're added via `cluster.add(atom.group)` but not into `dayBlocks`/`_pickables`, so 5.6.1's scoped collector already excludes them — keep it that way and **also** honor 5.4.8's explicit flag as defense-in-depth if the collector ever broadens). For padding cells (5.2.4): when created, set `userData.nonInteractive = true` (or omit from the interactive target set) and skip them in `_collectClickTargets`/the cached set. Ensure the halo `Mesh`es (WordWeaverScene.js:871-877) and label backings aren't accidentally added to the pick set.
- **Edge cases:** A real day block behind an atom or padding cell must still be pickable (don't let a non-interactive object block the ray if it's `raycast`-noop'd — a noop'd object isn't tested, so the ray passes through to the day, which is the desired behavior); if 5.2.4 makes padding cells *visible but dim*, they still must not hover/focus; the `eventGlow`/`noteSurface` of a real block stay interactive (they resolve to the dayBlock) — only padding/decoration is excluded.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Decorations and out-of-month filler that grab hover/clicks would create dead zones and confusion; the contract keeps every interactive pixel meaningful (§5.7).
- **Data flow notes:** Target-set construction filters out non-interactive objects; raycast tests only interactive day blocks + labels.
- **Testing notes:** A raycast aimed at an atom in front of a day returns the **day**; padding cells (if present) produce no hover/focus; halos/background never pick; real blocks (including their glow/note surfaces) still resolve to the dayBlock.
- **Performance notes:** Excluding decorations/padding shrinks the raycast set (a minor 5.6.2 win) and prevents wasted hover churn.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Identical; touch and mouse both route past decorations to real days.
- **Integration points:** §5.7 line 618-619, §5.5 line 562, 5.4.8 (atom contract), 5.2.4 (padding cells), 5.6.1 (scoped set).
- **Status:** **Implicit (atoms already excluded) / pending (padding).** Atoms aren't in the year-ring target set today (satisfied implicitly); there are no padding cells yet (5.2.4), but any added must be non-interactive; 5.4.8's explicit contract isn't yet enforced on the consumer side. **Next:** honor 5.4.8 explicitly, mark/skip any 5.2.4 padding cells, keep halos/background out of the pick set.

## 5.6.9 — Keyboard navigation (arrows / Enter / Escape)

- **Purpose:** Implement the §5.7 line 621-624 desktop keyboard navigation — **arrow keys** move focus between adjacent day blocks, **Enter** focuses the keyboard-focused day, and **Escape** steps the camera **month-overview → year-overview** — giving non-pointer access to the 3D navigation (accessibility) alongside the WASD fly (5.5.6).
- **Dependencies:** 5.2 (grid adjacency — the cluster's 7-column layout defines "adjacent"), 5.5 (`focusOnDay`/`focusOnMonth`/`focusOverview` the keys call), 5.5.6 (the single keyboard handler + text-input guard), 5.5.10 (Escape's camera reset half), 5.6.5 (Enter == click/focus); §5.7 line 621-624.
- **Acceptance criteria:** With the 3D canvas focused and no text input active (the 5.5.6 guard), **Left/Right** move the keyboard-focused day to the previous/next day, **Up/Down** move by a week (±7 in the cluster grid) — crossing month-cluster boundaries sensibly (end of a month → next month's first day, or document a within-cluster clamp); **Enter** calls `focusOnDay` on the keyboard-focused day (same as a click, 5.6.5); **Escape** steps back one level — if a day is focused → month overview, if at month → year overview (`focusOverview`, 5.5.3/5.5.10); the keyboard-focused day has a **visible focus indicator** (reusing the hover/focus visual, 5.6.3) distinct from pointer hover; this shares the single keyboard handler (5.5.6), not a new `window` listener.
- **Implementation notes:** Maintain a `_keyboardFocusIndex` (or a reference into `dayBlocks`/the current cluster grid); map arrows to grid moves using the cluster's `col`/`row` math (WordWeaverScene.js:917-921) and `findDayBlock` (WordWeaverScene.js:1007); on move, apply the focus-indicator visual (5.6.3 `setHover`-style or a dedicated `setKeyboardFocus`) and optionally scroll/`focusOnDay` to keep it in view; Enter → `focusOnDay` (5.6.5 path, emit `dayFocused`); Escape → step level (track current level: day-focused vs month vs year) calling `focusOnMonth`/`focusOverview`. Add these to the **single** keyboard handler from 5.5.6 (with the text-input guard) — do **not** register a competing `window` listener.
- **Edge cases:** Arrowing past the end of a month/year — define wrap vs clamp (recommend cross into the adjacent month cluster for a continuous feel, matching the ring); Escape when nothing is focused = stay at year overview (no-op); the keyboard focus indicator must not be confused with pointer hover (both could be visible — define precedence); reduced motion snaps the Enter/Escape camera moves (5.5.10); the guard must prevent arrows from navigating days while the user is editing text in a panel.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Keyboard nav is an accessibility requirement and a power-user affordance; a clear focus ring + Enter-to-commit + Escape-to-back-out mirrors standard app navigation and complements the spatial fly controls.
- **Data flow notes:** Key → grid move → keyboard-focus index → focus indicator; Enter → `focusOnDay`; Escape → level step → camera (5.5).
- **Testing notes:** Arrows move the focus indicator across adjacent days (and across month boundaries per the chosen rule); Enter focuses the day (camera moves, `dayFocused` emitted); Escape steps day→month→year; arrows don't fire while typing in a panel; the indicator is visible and distinct.
- **Performance notes:** Negligible (index math + one visual toggle per key).
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Desktop only (physical keyboard); mobile uses tap/overlay.
- **Integration points:** §5.7 line 621-624, 5.2 (grid adjacency/`findDayBlock`), 5.5.3/5.5.10 (Escape camera), 5.5.6 (single handler + guard), 5.6.3 (focus visual), 5.6.5 (Enter == focus).
- **Status:** **Missing.** Only fly keys (WASD/Q-E) exist; no arrow day-focus nav, no Enter-focus, no Escape-to-overview. **Next:** grid-adjacency arrow nav + Enter (→`focusOnDay`) + Escape (→level step) in the single keyboard handler, with a visible keyboard-focus indicator.

## 5.6.10 — §13.2 bus routing, reduced-motion, close-out & Phase-5 interaction convergence

- **Purpose:** Close Phase 5's interaction thread (and the phase): route clicks/focus through the **Phase-2 §13.2 bus** instead of the constructor `onNodeClick` callback (consuming 5.5.9), apply **reduced-motion** to hover lift / focus moves, **disposition the two pick systems**, and document the Phase-5 interaction model — so the 3D interaction is converged, accessible, bus-coherent, and consistent with the Phase-4/5 single-source/single-bus discipline.
- **Dependencies:** all prior 5.6 tasks, 5.5.9 (the `dayFocused`/`monthFocused`/`navigateTo` wiring this consumes), 5.5.10 (reduced-motion gate), 5.1.9 (canonical bus), 5.4.8 (atom contract finalized in 5.6.8); §13.2 line 1418/1101-1102, §23.3 line 1664, §5.7.
- **Acceptance criteria:** A pick that focuses a day/month routes through the **canonical bus** — emitting `dayFocused`/`monthFocused` (5.5.9) and opening the day panel via a bus event (5.6.5), **not** the constructor `onNodeClick` callback (which is removed or reduced to an internal adapter); opening/closing the day panel and 2D↔3D nav stay consistent (a 2D `navigateTo` → 3D focus and a 3D click → 2D/top-bar update both work, no feedback loop, 5.5.9's guard); under `prefers-reduced-motion` the hover **Y-lift** (5.6.3) is omitted and Enter/Escape/click **camera moves snap** (5.5.10); the **two pick systems** (year-ring day blocks vs satellite-timeline `_pickables`) are dispositioned (scoped per mode, 5.6.1) and documented; pointer listeners are added/removed on scene mount/dispose (already correct — WordWeaverScene.js:137-138/761-762 — verified); a short Phase-5 interaction close-out notes the resolved threads (hover/tooltip/click/label/keyboard, bus routing, atom exclusion).
- **Implementation notes:** Replace the `onNodeClick(...)` calls (WordWeaverScene.js:691-708) with bus emits (`dayFocused`, and a `openDayPanel`/day-detail event the panel subscribes to) on the **Phase-2 `src/utils/EventBus.js`** (the 5.1.9 convergence target) — keep a thin internal adapter only if an external embed still needs the callback, but the **canonical** path is the bus; ensure the 5.5.9 `navigateTo` listener and these emits don't loop (commands vs results). Apply the scene-level reduced-motion flag (5.5.10 / 5.4.6) to the hover lift and the focus-move snap. Confirm the two-pick-system disposition from 5.6.1 is documented (which mode uses which). Verify listener teardown on dispose.
- **Edge cases:** A click-driven `dayFocused` must not re-enter `navigateTo` → `focusOnDay` (re-entrancy guard, 5.5.9); the day panel opened via bus must populate from the model (no fabricated note, 5.6.5); reduced-motion must still allow interaction (only motion is reduced — hover state/tooltip/keyboard nav remain); an external consumer relying on `onNodeClick` must be migrated or adapted (grep for who passes it); double mount/dispose must not double-bind pointer listeners.
- **UI/UX considerations:** Bus routing is what makes a 3D click update the 2D views and top bar (and vice-versa) — the coherence the whole Inkling/WordWeaver dual-mode model needs; reduced-motion keeps interaction usable for motion-sensitive users without removing function.
- **Data flow notes:** Pick → (bus) `dayFocused`/`monthFocused` + (bus) open-day-panel; (bus) `navigateTo` → focus; reduced-motion flag → hover lift / focus snap.
- **Testing notes:** A 3D day click emits `dayFocused` on the Phase-2 bus and updates the top bar/2D; a second click opens the model-backed day panel; a 2D `navigateTo` moves the 3D camera; no `navigateTo`↔`dayFocused` loop; reduced motion drops the hover lift and snaps focus; pointer listeners are bound once and removed on dispose; the two pick systems are mode-scoped and documented.
- **Performance notes:** Bus emit per interaction (not per frame) — negligible; reduced-motion snap skips lerp work.
- **Mobile vs desktop:** Bus/reduced-motion identical; keyboard nav (5.6.9) desktop-only; tooltip (5.6.4) desktop-only.
- **Integration points:** §13.2 line 1418/1101-1102, §23.3 line 1664, §5.7, 5.5.9 (emit/consume), 5.5.10/5.4.6 (reduced-motion), 5.1.9 (canonical bus), 5.6.1 (pick-system disposition), 5.6.5 (panel via bus).
- **Status:** **Open (bus + close-out).** Interaction routes through a constructor `onNodeClick` callback (not the §13.2 bus); no reduced-motion handling; two pick systems coexist unscoped; the day panel uses a fabricated note. **Next:** route clicks/focus/panel through the canonical bus (consume 5.5.9), apply reduced-motion to hover/focus, disposition the two pick systems, document the Phase-5 interaction close-out.
